### [1] [From Affect to Complex Behavior: Advancing Multimodal Human-Centered AI at the 10th ABAW Workshop & Competition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27451)
*Dimitrios Kollias,Panagiotis Tzirakis,Alan Cowen,Stefanos Zafeiriou,Irene Kotsia,Eric Granger,Marco Pedersoli,Simon Bacon,Jens Madsen,Soufiane Belharbi,Muhammad Haseeb Aslam,Chunchang Shao,Guanyu Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 第十届ABAW研讨会与竞赛（CVPR 2026）延续双轨结构（竞赛+论文），竞赛涵盖连续情感估计、离散情感识别、情绪模仿强度估计、矛盾/犹豫识别及暴力检测等多个挑战；论文轨道涵盖姿态运动估计、情感建模、多模态学习、基准数据集等方向，旨在推动多模态人本AI系统发展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推动真实非约束环境下人类情感与行为的建模、分析与理解研究，为下一代多模态人本AI系统提供基准测试平台。

Method: 采用双轨结构：(1) 竞赛轨道——基于大规模野外数据集设置多个挑战任务，包括连续情感（效价-唤醒度）估计、离散情感（表情+动作单元）识别、情绪模仿强度估计、矛盾/犹豫识别以及细粒度暴力检测；(2) 论文轨道——征集涵盖姿态运动与行为估计、情感建模与多模态学习、基准数据集与评估协议、公平性鲁棒性及部署等主题的论文。

Result: 为各类情感与行为分析任务提供了全面的野外基准测试，展示了当前最先进方法在该领域的表现水平。

Conclusion: 第十届ABAW研讨会与竞赛继续作为情感计算领域的关键平台，促进了基准测试、合作与创新，推动了多模态人本AI系统的发展。

Abstract: The 10th Affective & Behavior Analysis in-the-Wild (ABAW) Workshop and Competition, held at CVPR 2026, continues to advance research on modelling, analysis, understanding of human affect and behavior in real-world, unconstrained environments. The workshop maintains its dual structure, comprising both a competition and a paper track. The ABAW Competition introduces a diverse set of challenges targeting key aspects of affective and behavioral understanding, including continuous affect (valence-arousal) estimation, discrete affect (expression and action unit) recognition, as well as more complex behavior analysis tasks, such as emotional mimicry intensity estimation, ambivalence/hesitancy recognition and fine-grained violence detection. These challenges are built upon large-scale in-the-wild datasets, providing comprehensive benchmarks for state-of-the-art approaches. In parallel, the paper track presents a wide range of contributions spanning pose, motion & behavior estimation, affect modelling & multimodal learning, benchmarks, datasets & evaluation protocols, fairness, robustness & deployment. Overall, the 10th ABAW Workshop and Competition continues to serve as a key platform for benchmarking, collaboration and innovation, shaping the development of next-generation multimodal, human-centered AI systems.

</details>


### [2] [Fine-Tuning Vision-Language Models for Understanding Current Damage and Scoring Priority with Quality Guard Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27452)
*Takato Yasuno*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于微调视觉语言模型（VLM）的桥梁损伤自动化评估与修复优先级评分方法。通过微调LLaVA-1.5-7B模型，结合规则引擎计算五级修复优先级，实验表明2k训练样本即可达到近最优验证损失，而3k样本在语义相似度上表现最佳。推理优化使每张图像处理时间降至10.06秒（减少70.2%），并引入两阶段质量守卫机制过滤低质量输出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 日本桥梁检查每五年需进行强制性目视评估，但不同工程师给出的定性损伤评级（a-e级）存在显著评分者间变异性，成为基础设施一致性管理的关键障碍。同时，熟练工程师的老龄化进一步威胁检查能力，因此需要一种自动化、标准化的桥梁损伤评估方法。

Method: 使用QLoRA微调LLaVA-1.5-7B模型，在最多4,000对桥梁损伤图像与检测文本记录上训练，并在800张图像的固定测试集上评估。模型输出自然语言描述（识别结构构件和损伤模式），规则评分引擎据此计算五级修复优先级指数。进行渐进训练研究（1k/2k/3k/4k样本），并采用torch.compile()和批处理（batch_size=8）优化推理。引入两阶段质量守卫机制，使用微调后的Swallow-8B SLM在优先级评分前过滤低质量VLM输出。

Result: 2k训练样本在仅2.9小时训练后达到近最优验证损失；超过2k后，训练样本每翻倍验证损失改进不超过0.2%，呈现明显收益递减。语义相似度在3k样本时达到峰值（0.6909），在4k时下降（0.6739），表明质量精选的中等规模数据优于更大但更噪杂的语料。推理优化实现每张图像10.06秒处理时间，相比未优化基线减少70.2%。

Conclusion: 该方法有助于桥梁检查中的数据治理，降低评分者间变异性，并提供AI辅助分诊以增强专家工程师的检查流程。两阶段质量守卫机制可有效防止来自损坏或无法识别图像的错误评分。

Abstract: Bridge inspection in Japan requires mandatory visual assessments every five years, yet qualitative damage ratings (levels a-e) assigned by different engineers exhibit significant inter-rater variability -- a critical barrier to consistent infrastructure management. The aging of skilled engineers further threatens inspection capacity. This paper presents a methodology for automating bridge damage understanding and repair priority scoring using fine-tuned Vision-Language Models (VLMs).
  We fine-tune LLaVA-1.5-7B with QLoRA on up to 4,000 paired bridge damage images and inspection text records, then evaluate on a fixed test set of 800 images. The model outputs natural language descriptions identifying structural members and damage patterns, from which a rule-based scoring engine calculates a five-level repair priority index. A progressive training study (1k/2k/3k/4k samples) reveals that 2k training samples achieve near-optimal validation loss in only 2.9 hours of training; beyond 2k, validation loss improves by no more than 0.2% per doubling of training samples, exhibiting clear diminishing returns. Furthermore, semantic similarity on the held-out test set peaks at 3k (0.6909) and degrades at 4k (0.6739), indicating that quality-curated mid-scale data outperforms larger but noisier corpora. Inference optimization combining torch.compile() and batch processing (batch_size=8) achieves 10.06 seconds per image -- a 70.2% reduction over the unoptimized baseline.
  Our approach contributes to data governance in bridge inspection, reduces inter-rater variability, and provides AI-assisted triage to augment expert engineers in inspection workflows. Furthermore, we introduce a two-stage Quality Guard using a fine-tuned Swallow-8B SLM to reject low-quality VLM outputs before priority scoring, preventing spurious scores from damaged or unrecognised images.

</details>


### [3] [Generic Interpretation Approach for Transformer Models Incorporating Heterogenous Attention Structures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27458)
*Yongjin Cui,Xiaohui Fan,Huajun Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对异构注意力结构Transformer模型的解释方法，并通过实验对代表性模型进行语义和逻辑层面的解释分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer在AI和智能体领域发展迅速，异构注意力结构（如co-attention）是模型实现复杂功能和多模态信息集成的基础。出于研究和政策需求，解释具有异构注意力结构的Transformer模型是一项重要任务，而不同来源信息的融合带来了新的解释挑战。

Method: 将Transformer注意力结构分为同质和异构两类，针对异构注意力结构提出专用的解释方法，并建立实验分析范式，对代表性模型进行语义解释和逻辑解释。

Result: 基于所提出的解释方法和实验分析范式，成功对代表性Transformer模型的操作机制进行了语义和逻辑层面的解释。

Conclusion: 异构注意力结构是Transformer实现复杂功能的基础，所提出的解释方法能够有效揭示其内部运行机制。

Abstract: Transformer has significantly propelled the development of artificial intelligence, and certainly the development of agents as well. We categorize attention structures of Transformer into two types based on the source of the input information: homogenous and heterogenous attention structures. Heterogenous attention structures, with co-attention as a typical example, process information from different sources. Heterogenous attention structure is the foundation for Transformer models to achieve more complex functions and integrate more modal information. Whether for research purposes or policy requirements, the interpretation of Transformer models with heterogenous attention structures is an important task. The fusion of information from different sources brings new challenges. Our work mainly includes two parts: method and experimentation. In terms of method, we propose an interpretation method for Transformer models with heterogenous attention structures. In terms of experimentation, based on our experimental analysis paradigm, we interpret the operating mechanisms of representative models, conduct semantic interpretation and logical interpretation.

</details>


### [4] [D$^2$Turb: Depth-Aware Simulation and Decoupled Learning for Single-Frame Atmospheric Turbulence Mitigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27460)
*Zixiao Hu,Tianyu Li,Guoqing Wang,Wei Li,Guoguo Xin,Xun Liu,Peng Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: D²Turb是一个统一框架，通过引入深度感知湍流合成协议和显式解耦的恢复策略（纹理去模糊与几何校正），在物理一致的仿真驱动下实现了大气湍流图像复原的SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的端到端方法在平场仿真上训练，难以平衡纹理恢复与几何校正；而单帧大气湍流缓解本质上具有病态性，空间变化模糊与非刚性几何畸变耦合使得问题更加复杂。

Method: 提出D²Turb框架：（1）Depth-Aware Turbulence Synthesis协议，将场景深度融入相位-空间公式，生成深度相关的退化并提供倾斜监督信号；（2）解耦为两个交互阶段——纹理去模糊（恢复细节同时保留几何畸变）和几何校正（预测稠密流场进行空间矫正）；（3）自适应结构先验注入（ASPI）机制，动态传递深层结构表征以指导稠密流预测，缓解级联设计中的信息碎片化。

Result: 在合成和真实数据集上均达到SOTA性能，纹理恢复和几何保真度均有一致提升。

Conclusion: D²Turb通过物理仿真与显式解耦恢复的有效结合，为单帧大气湍流缓解提供了一种性能优越的统一解决方案。

Abstract: Single-frame atmospheric turbulence mitigation is inherently ill-posed due to spatially varying blur coupled with non-rigid geometric distortion. Existing end-to-end approaches trained on flat-field simulations often struggle to balance texture recovery with geometric rectification. To overcome this limitation, we propose D$^2$Turb, a unified framework that bridges physics-grounded simulation with explicitly decoupled restoration. First, we introduce a Depth-Aware Turbulence Synthesis protocol that incorporates scene depth into the phase-to-space formulation. This generates physically consistent, depth-dependent degradations and provides a crucial intermediate tilt supervision signal for disentangled learning. Building upon this simulation engine, D$^2$Turb decomposes restoration into two interactive stages: texture deblurring and geometric rectification. The texture deblurring stage employs a deblurring backbone to recover fine-grained details while preserving geometric distortion for the subsequent rectification stage. To mitigate the information fragmentation commonly observed in cascaded designs, we further propose an Adaptive Structural Prior Injection (ASPI) mechanism that dynamically transfers deep structural representations from the deblurring module to guide dense flow prediction for spatial unwarping. Extensive experiments demonstrate that D$^2$Turb achieves state-of-the-art performance on both synthetic and real-world datasets, with consistent improvements in both texture recovery and geometric fidelity. Our code and pre-trained models are publicly available at https://github.com/HertzDot222/D2Turb.

</details>


### [5] [Beyond Motion Primitives: Behavioral Activity Recognition from Head-Mounted IMU](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27464)
*Chung-Ta Huang,Leopold Das,Jeffrey Zhou,Faizaan Siddique,Julia Seungjoo Baek,Serena Liu,Andrew Rusli,Todd Y. Zhou,Freddy Yu,Sinclair Hansen,Ziling Hu,Arnav Sharma,Mengyu Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出HiT-HAR，一个70.3万参数的层次化模型，利用头戴式IMU传感器将运动基元识别提升至行为级识别（5类动作和8类场景），基于16万样本的Ego4D数据集，并通过每类可分性分析绘制了头戴式IMU的可观测性边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AR智能眼镜需要持续的行为上下文以提供主动辅助，但最实用的常开传感器——头戴式IMU仅能检测行走、站立等运动基元。本文旨在将识别能力从运动基元提升至行为级识别，以更好地支持AR应用需求。

Method: 构建了16万样本的Ego4D数据集，采用四层质量保证框架覆盖8个活动场景；提出HiT-HAR，一个70.3万参数的层次化模型，利用时间上下文和场景结构进行行为识别；通过每类可分性分析绘制头戴式IMU的可观测性边界。

Result: HiT-HAR在5类动作和8类场景识别任务上均优于先前的头戴式IMU模型；发现利用时间上下文和场景结构的架构选择优于单纯扩大模型规模；明确了哪些行为类别可靠可观测（移动）、哪些受益于时间上下文（物体传递、任务操作）、以及哪些存在场景依赖的信号重叠挑战。

Conclusion: 头戴式IMU结合合适的层次化架构能够实现行为级识别，但不同行为类别的可观测性存在差异，架构设计应优先考虑时间上下文和场景结构的利用而非单纯扩大模型参数规模。

Abstract: AR smart glasses need continuous behavioral context to offer proactive assistance, yet their most practical always-on sensor, the head-mounted Inertial Measurement Unit (IMU), detects only motion primitives such as walking or standing. We push beyond motion primitives to behavioral-level recognition, defining five categories that balance AR application need with sensor observability. To this end, we construct a 160K-sample Ego4D dataset with a four-tier quality assurance framework spanning 8 activity scenarios, and propose HiT-HAR, a 703K-parameter hierarchical model that outperforms prior head-mounted IMU models on five-class action and eight-class scenario recognition. We further map the observability frontier of head-mounted IMU through per-class separability analysis, identifying which behavioral categories are reliably observable (Locomotion), which benefit from temporal context (Object Transfer, Task Operation), and where scenario-dependent signal overlap poses remaining challenges. Our results indicate that architectural choices exploiting temporal context and scenario structure outperform simply scaling model size. The code and dataset are publicly available at https://github.com/Harvard-AI-and-Robotics-Lab/HiT-HAR.

</details>


### [6] [Diffusion-Based Ukrainian Handwritten Text Generation with Cross-Domain Style Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27487)
*Andrii Ahitoliev,Pavlo Berezin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文构建了包含126,177张图像的乌克兰手写字数据集，并在不修改架构的情况下将扩散模型DiffusionPen从拉丁语系迁移到西里尔语系，通过跨语言、零样本和少样本三种设置验证了该模型能够生成风格一致且可读的手写文本，表明少样本潜扩散模型可泛化到非拉丁语系领域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 西里尔文字（尤其是乌克兰语）缺乏大规模带作者标注的数据集，且现有手写文本生成模型主要针对拉丁语系，在非拉丁语系上的泛化能力尚不明确。

Method: 构建乌克兰手写字数据集（126,177张图像，308位作者），采用连通分量分割、质量过滤和欠表示字符过采样；在不修改架构的条件下，将DiffusionPen（基于MobileNetV2三元组损失风格编码器 + CANINE条件潜扩散U-Net）重新训练于该数据集。

Result: 模型在跨语言迁移（IAM英文样本）、零样本迁移（20世纪初乌克兰手稿）和少样本模仿（当代作者）三种设置下均能生成可读且风格一致的手写文字图像。

Conclusion: 少样本潜扩散模型能够泛化到拉丁语系之外，所发布的数据集、模型和评估协议可作为可重复基准，推动其他低资源书写系统的风格化手写文本生成研究。

Abstract: Handwritten text generation (HTG) conditioned on writer style has been widely studied for Latin scripts, but remains underexplored for low-resource and non-Latin writing systems, leaving open how well existing models generalise beyond the Latin domain. Cyrillic, particularly Ukrainian, lacks both large-scale writer-labeled datasets and empirical evidence of such generalisation. To address this gap, we construct a Ukrainian handwritten word dataset of 126,177 images from 308 writers using connected-component segmentation, quality filtering, and targeted oversampling of underrepresented Ukrainian characters. We retrain DiffusionPen, a MobileNetV2 triplet-loss style encoder with a CANINE-conditioned latent diffusion U-Net, on this dataset without architectural modification, testing direct transfer from Latin to Cyrillic. We evaluate cross-domain style transfer in three settings: cross-lingual transfer from IAM English samples, zero-shot transfer to an early 20th-century Ukrainian manuscript, and few-shot imitation of contemporary writers. The model produces legible, style-consistent word images, indicating that few-shot latent diffusion models generalize beyond the Latin-script domain. We release the dataset, trained models, and evaluation protocol as a reproducible benchmark for writer-aware Cyrillic HTG, providing a foundation for extending stylized HTG to other underrepresented writing systems.

</details>


### [7] [Representation-Conditioned Diffusion Models for Guided Training Data Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27495)
*Nithesh Chandher Karthikeyan,Jonas Unger,Gabriel Eilertsen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究使用基于表示条件（DINOv2、DINOv3、CLIP）的潜在扩散模型生成合成图像数据集，用于训练分类器。结果表明，该方法在ImageNet100上比类别条件生成高出10.76个百分点，且通过扩大合成数据集规模，性能甚至超越真实数据训练的模型（+2.0个百分点），同时还能用于数据增强和样本过滤。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习应用中数据可用性仍是关键瓶颈，大规模数据集收集、整理和标注成本高昂，限制了监督学习的可扩展性和适用性。本文旨在探索使用生成式深度学习产生的合成图像数据集来替代或增强真实数据。

Method: 使用潜在扩散模型，以DINOv2、DINOv3和CLIP学习到的表示为条件进行图像生成；与传统的类别条件生成进行对比；评估合成数据对分类模型训练的影响；探索合成数据用于数据增强和样本过滤的方法。

Result: 表示条件生成在ImageNet100上比类别条件生成高出10.76个百分点（top-1准确率）；扩大合成数据集后，性能超越真实数据训练模型2.0个百分点；合成数据用于增强时优于传统增强方法；条件空间可用于样本过滤以提升训练价值。

Conclusion: 表示条件扩散模型为增强、补充或潜在替代大规模视觉学习任务中的真实数据集提供了一种有前景的方法。

Abstract: Data availability remains a critical bottleneck in many deep learning applications. Large-scale datasets are often expensive to collect, curate and annotate, which can limit the scalability and applicability of supervised learning methods. In this work, we evaluate the classification performance of models trained on synthetic image datasets produced by generative deep learning. In particular, we use latent diffusion models conditioned on learned representations from DINOv2, DINOv3, and CLIP. Our results demonstrates that this representation-conditioned formulation significantly outperforms class-conditioned generation by a large margin (+10.76 p.p. top-1 accuracy on ImageNet100), by improving sample quality and mode coverage. Furthermore, by scaling the size of the synthetic dataset, we are able to outperform a classifier trained on the real data (+2.0 p.p top-1 accuracy).
  We also demonstrate how generated images can be used for augmentation purposes, outperforming classical augmentation methods, and how the conditioning space can be used for sample filtering to further improve training value. Collectively, these findings highlight that representation-conditioned diffusion models provide a promising approach for augmenting, complementing, or potentially replacing real-world datasets in large-scale visual learning tasks.

</details>


### [8] [Clinical Validation of the Melanoscope AI Mobile Dermoscopy Clinical Decision Support System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27561)
*Elena Sergeevna Kozachok,Sergey Sergeevich Seregin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文开发了Melanoscope AI皮肤镜辅助诊断系统，采用级联深度学习模型与注意力图可视化方法，并在俄罗斯门诊进行前瞻性单中心验证。对176例患者的前瞻性验证结果显示，与专家评估一致率达88.6%，恶性病变中无假阴性，特异性88.3%，提出了基于IoU的量化可解释性评估方法和三区患者分流算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 俄罗斯地区皮肤科医生短缺限制了皮肤恶性病变的筛查覆盖率，而现有的移动皮肤镜临床决策支持系统在模型可解释性和标准化患者分流方面仍存在关键障碍，亟需开发兼具可解释性和临床实用性的辅助诊断系统。

Method: 采用两阶段级联分类架构处理皮肤镜图像；使用注意力 rollout（ViT和Swin）和Grad-CAM（ConvNeXt和EfficientNetV2）进行注意力图可视化；通过基于IoU的定量一致性评估比较激活图与专家标注；设计三区患者路由算法（阈值：P<0.15 / 0.15-0.50 / >=0.50）；在俄罗斯奥廖尔开展四次"黑色素瘤日"前瞻性单中心验证（2025年6月至2026年4月）。

Result: 176例患者：与专家评估一致率88.6%；5例恶性病变中无假阴性（95% CI: 47.8-100.0%）；特异性88.3%；组织学确诊3例黑色素瘤和2例基底细胞癌，6例发育不良痣纳入随访。平均IoU值（n=180）：ViT - 0.69；Swin - 0.64；ConvNeXt - 0.53；EfficientNetV2 - 0.51。

Conclusion: 系统未出现假阴性，特异性达88.3%，支持用于筛查场景。集成的级联分类、注意力图可视化与IoU评估、三区分流算法提供了可重复、可解释的临床决策支持，且可适应不同资源水平。

Abstract: Introduction. Early detection of malignant skin lesions is critical for prognosis, yet dermatologist shortages in Russian regions limit screening coverage. Mobile dermoscopy clinical decision support systems (CDSS) offer a promising approach, with model interpretability and standardised patient routing remaining key barriers to adoption.
  Aim. To develop a quantitative interpretability assessment method for cascade deep learning models and a three-zone patient routing algorithm, and to conduct a preliminary single-centre prospective clinical validation of the Melanoscope AI CDSS in Russian outpatient practice.
  Material and methods. Two-stage cascade classification of dermoscopic images; attention map visualisation (attention rollout for ViT and Swin; Grad-CAM for ConvNeXt and EfficientNetV2); quantitative IoU-based agreement assessment between activation maps and expert annotations; prospective single-centre validation across four "Melanoma Day" sessions (Orel, Russia, June 2025 - April 2026).
  Results. On 176 patients: agreement with expert assessment 88.6%; no false negatives among 5 malignant lesions (95% CI: 47.8-100.0%); specificity 88.3%. Three melanomas and two basal cell carcinomas were histologically confirmed; six dysplastic naevi placed under follow-up. Mean IoU (n=180): ViT - 0.69; Swin - 0.64; ConvNeXt - 0.53; EfficientNetV2 - 0.51. Routing thresholds: P<0.15 / 0.15-0.50 / >=0.50.
  Conclusion. No false negatives were observed; specificity was 88.3%, supporting screening use. The integrated cascade classification, attention map visualisation with IoU assessment, and three-zone routing provide reproducible, interpretable clinical decision support adaptable to varying resource levels.

</details>


### [9] [What-If World: A Causal Benchmark for General World Models in Embodied Scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27589)
*Kunlin Cai,Rui Song,Jinghuai Zhang,Kaiyuan Zhang,Pranav Bodapati,Alicia Yu,Fnu Suya,Mohammad Rostami,Jiaqi Ma,Yuan Tian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出What-If World基准，包含319对提示词对（基于nuScenes和DROID），通过改变单一物理变量检验视频生成模型是否具备因果理解能力。使用APEO四部分评分体系评估，结果发现SOTA模型在配对得分上均低于52%，开源模型约28%，表明模型当前无法可靠支持动作条件仿真或基于模型的规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频生成模型评估仅关注单视频的视觉合理性，忽略了模型是否对输入变化作出符合物理规律的响应（即因果干预测试）。在机器人操作和自动驾驶等应用中，模型需要能够根据场景中物理变量的变化生成相应的视频输出，而现有基准无法检测这种失败模式。

Method: 构建What-If World基准：(1) 从nuScenes（驾驶）和DROID（操作）数据集中选取真实帧，设计319对提示词，每对仅改变一个物理变量（共六类：物体存在、位置、颜色、大小、速度、光照）；(2) 提出APEO评分：Adherence（视频是否遵循提示）、Physics（物理一致性）、Environment（共享场景保持）、Outcome（最终差异是否正确）。

Result: 9个SOTA模型中，无模型配对得分超过52%，开源模型集中在28%附近。模型表现与干预的视觉显著性相关而非物理可解性：视觉微妙的干预得分低至14.2%，视觉显著的干预达40.4%。所有模型在大量因果干预上失败。

Conclusion: 当前视频生成模型尚不能可靠支持动作条件仿真或基于模型的规划，因为它们在因果物理推理上存在系统性缺陷。需要新的评估范式来推动模型从"视觉合理"走向"因果正确"。

Abstract: Video generation models are increasingly used as world simulators for tasks like driving and robotic manipulation. What matters in these settings is not whether a single video looks right, but whether the model's output changes when its input changes. We test this by giving a model two prompts describing the same scene with one physical detail varied, and checking whether the two videos diverge the way physics predicts. The wording difference between the prompts is small by design, since only one variable is changed, but the correct physical difference is not. A model that misses this can still produce two videos that each look plausible individually, and existing benchmarks score videos one at a time and cannot detect this failure. We introduce What-If World, 319 such prompt pairs built on real frames from nuScenes and DROID, organized by a taxonomy of six physical variables shared across driving and manipulation. Each pair is scored with APEO, a four-part rubric checking whether each video follows its prompt (Adherence), is physically consistent (Physics), preserves the shared scene (Environment), and ends in the correct difference (Outcome). Across nine state-of-the-art models, no system exceeds 52% on the paired score, and open-source models cluster near 28%. Every model tested fails on a large fraction of causal interventions, indicating substantial room before these models can reliably support action-conditioned simulation or model-based planning. Where models do score well, performance appears to track the visual prominence of the intervention rather than the tractability of its underlying physics. Some visually subtle interventions score as low as 14.2%, while visually pronounced ones reach 40.4%.

</details>


### [10] [ForestHG-Trace: Traceable Long-Horizon Ecological Reasoning over Large-Scale Forest Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27590)
*Zihang Cheng,Duanchu Wang,Cheng Li,Jing Huang,Huanzhao Fu,Di Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ForestHG-Trace是一个面向森林遥感问答的框架，将NEON森林场景表示为生态超图，通过LLM引导的智能体调用确定性工具进行多步推理，生成可重放的执行轨迹和证据记录。同时构建了ForestTraceQA基准，实验表明该框架在准确性和执行忠实度上显著优于单步基线和场景图方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 遥感问答中，大规模森林场景的生态分析涉及多步过滤、数值聚合、邻域推理和可验证证据，传统直接语义预测方法难以胜任这些需要长程推理的任务。

Method: 将NEON森林场景建模为生态超图，包含树木实例、空间单元、语义组和邻域关系等超边以支持高阶推理；LLM引导的智能体通过阅读、过滤、扩展、聚合、比较和审计等确定性工具生成可重放的执行轨迹和证据记录。

Result: 构建了ForestTraceQA可执行基准，实验表明ForestHG-Trace在答案准确性和执行忠实度上大幅优于单步基线和场景图智能体方法，并指出执行深度是长程生态QA的主要瓶颈。

Conclusion: 基于超图的生态场景表示结合LLM引导的确定性工具调用，能有效提升森林遥感问答中长程推理的准确性和可追溯性。

Abstract: Remote sensing question answering (RS-QA) often requires more than direct semantic prediction, especially in large-scale forest scenes where ecological analysis involves multi-step filtering, numerical aggregation, neighborhood reasoning, and verifiable evidence. We introduce ForestHG-Trace, a framework for traceable long-horizon ecological reasoning over forest environments. It represents multimodal NEON forest scenes as ecological hypergraphs, where tree instances, spatial units, semantic groups, and neighborhood relations support higher-order reasoning beyond pairwise scene graphs. An LLM-guided agent then invokes deterministic tools for reading, filtering, expansion, aggregation, comparison, and auditing, producing replayable execution traces and compact evidence records rather than only free-form answers. We further construct ForestTraceQA, an executable benchmark for evaluating ecological QA across diverse task types and reasoning depths. Experiments show that ForestHG-Trace substantially improves answer accuracy and execution faithfulness over single-step baselines and scene-graph agents, while highlighting execution depth as the main bottleneck for long-horizon ecological QA.

</details>


### [11] [Hallucination Behavior in Multimodal LLMs Across Agricultural Image Interpretation and Generation Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27595)
*Partho Ghose,Al Bashir,Prem Raj,Azlan Zahid*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究系统评估了大语言模型（LLMs）在农业图像应用中的幻觉问题，涵盖图像到文本（作物/田地图像解读）和文本到图像（农业场景生成）两个方向。研究发现，在图像解读任务中，零样本准确率仅为63-75%，少样本提示可提升至86.8%，但仍存在误检和漏检；在文本生成图像任务中，GPT-5和Gemini 2.5 Flash等先进模型在宽松提示约束下可产生高达91%的生物不一致场景。研究揭示了LLMs在农业视觉推理和生成中的根本性缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLMs在农业成像应用中快速普及，但经常出现看似自信却偏离生物或环境现实的幻觉输出，可能导致错误的农学见解，亟需系统性评估和缓解策略。

Method: 研究采用双向评估框架：1）图像到文本方向：评估Gemma、LLAVA、Qwen、MiniCPM等模型解读作物/田地图像中生物与非生物胁迫的零样本和少样本表现；2）文本到图像方向：评估GPT-5、Gemini 2.5 Flash等模型根据描述性提示生成农业场景的生物学一致性。通过领域知识标准对多模态输出进行系统分类和分析。

Result: 图像解读：零样本准确率63-75%，少样本提示提升至86.8%但仍有残留幻觉；文本生成图像：GPT-5和Gemini 2.5 Flash在宽松提示下高达91%的场景存在生物不一致性，揭示了当前LLMs在农业视觉任务中的根本性弱点。

Conclusion: 该系统性评估为提升基于LLM的农业成像平台的可靠性和可信度提供了关键见解，表明当前模型在农业领域的视觉推理和生成能力存在显著不足，需要进一步发展以降低幻觉风险。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are being rapidly adopted in agricultural imaging applications, ranging from crop interpretation to synthetic field image generation. However, these models frequently exhibit hallucinations outputs that appear confident yet deviate from biological or environmental reality potentially leading to misinformed agronomic insights. This study investigates such hallucinations in two complementary directions: image-to-text, where LLMs interpret crop or field imagery to describe conditions such as biotic and abiotic stresses, and text-to-image, where models generate synthetic agricultural scenes based on descriptive prompts. We examine errors involving biological inconsistency, contextual inaccuracy, and agronomic implausibility, evaluating the outputs under domain-informed criteria across multiple imaging modalities. Our analysis identifies recurring hallucination patterns within both interpretive and generative tasks. In image interpretation, LLMs (e.g., Gemma, LLAVA, Qwen, and MiniCPM) achieved modest zero-shot accuracy (63 to 75 percent), whereas few-shot prompting improved performance up to 86.8 percent, exhibiting false detections and missed infections, indicating residual hallucination effects. In text-to-image tasks, advanced models such as GPT-5 and Gemini 2.5 Flash generate up to 91 percent biologically inconsistent scenes under relaxed prompt constraints, revealing fundamental weaknesses in current LLMs. This systematic assessment of visual reasoning and generation offers critical insights toward enhancing the reliability and trustworthiness of LLM-based agricultural imaging platforms.

</details>


### [12] [Not All NVFP4 QAT Recipes Are Equal: How Architecture and Scale Shape Model Quality for Anomaly Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27616)
*Zijian Du,Oleg Rybakov*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了模型架构、模型规模和FP4量化感知训练（QAT）方法在实时异常分割任务中的三向交互作用。结果表明，架构选择对量化鲁棒性影响最大，基于注意力的架构（如Swin Transformer）对QAT方法选择具有显著韧性，而CNN在大规模下受梯度量化噪声影响严重。Swin Transformer被推荐为FP4量化异常分割的首选架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实时异常分割需要高召回率和高效的低精度推理，但FP4量化对模型性能的影响尚缺乏系统研究，特别是架构、规模与QAT方法之间的交互关系不明确。

Method: 在脑肿瘤分割任务上，采用统一协议，对比多种模型架构（包括注意力和CNN）、不同模型规模以及多种FP4 QAT方法，并通过五折患者级交叉验证确保结果稳健性。

Result: 1) 架构选择对量化鲁棒性影响最大；2) 注意力架构对QAT方法选择高度鲁棒，CNN在大规模下受梯度量化噪声影响；3) 低容量时FP4可能离散化softmax注意力，高级QAT可避免；4) 大规模下高级QAT可缓解CNN的梯度量化噪声。

Conclusion: Swin Transformer在所有规模下均对QAT方法选择鲁棒，是FP4量化异常分割的推荐架构。

Abstract: Real-time anomaly segmentation demands both high recall and efficient low-precision inference. We study the three-way interaction of model architecture, model scale, and FP4 quantization-aware training (QAT) recipe on a recall-critical brain tumor segmentation task, evaluating multiple architectures, scales, and QAT recipes under a unified protocol. We find that architecture choice has the largest impact on quantization robustness, with attention-based architectures showing remarkable resilience to recipe choice while CNN degrades under gradient-quantizing recipes at larger scales. At low capacity, FP4 can discretize softmax attention, but advanced QAT recipes prevent this collapse. At larger scales, advanced recipes mitigate gradient quantization noise that degrades CNN quality. Five-fold patient-level cross-validation confirms these findings are robust to data partition. Our results show that the Swin Transformer is robust to QAT recipe choice across all scales, making it the recommended architecture for FP4-quantized anomaly segmentation.

</details>


### [13] [Tensor Memory: Fixed-Size Recurrent State for Long-Horizon Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27686)
*Kabir Swain,Sijie Han,Daniel Karl I. Weidele,Mauro Martino,Antonio Torralba*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Tensor Memory模块，用固定大小的3D记忆张量增强Transformer，实现与输入长度解耦的持久空间状态，在语言、图像和视频任务上提升长序列理解与遮挡推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer在处理视频时展平时空为长序列，注意力机制和KV缓存随序列长度增长，缺乏显式的持久空间状态，导致长程视频理解和遮挡推理困难。

Method: 设计一个轻量级Tensor Memory模块：token通过可微软写入将内容以高斯加权体积沉积到预测的连续3D位置；通过高效的局部交互算子和门控循环动力学更新记忆；通过连续采样和门控残差融合读取上下文。

Result: 在标准语言、图像和视频基准测试以及玩具诊断任务上评估，证明其能集成到标准Transformer训练流程，无需其他架构改动即可提升性能。

Conclusion: 固定大小的3D记忆张量有效解耦了状态容量与输入长度，同时保留了空间归纳偏置，增强了Transformer的长序列理解和遮挡敏感推理能力。

Abstract: Transformers process images and videos by flattening space and time into long token sequences. While attention and KV caching preserve past features, their memory grows with sequence length and they lack an explicit, persistent spatial state, making long-horizon video understanding and occlusion-sensitive reasoning difficult. We propose Tensor Memory, a lightweight module that augments Transformer blocks with a fixed-size recurrent 3D memory tensor: tokens write into a voxel grid via a differentiable soft write that deposits content as a Gaussian-weighted volume around a predicted continuous 3D location, the memory is updated with an efficient local interaction operator and gated recurrent dynamics, and tokens read back context via continuous sampling with gated residual fusion. Because the memory tensor has a constant size, Tensor Memory decouples state capacity from input length while preserving a spatial inductive bias. We evaluate the module on standard language, image, and video benchmarks and on a controlled toy diagnostic suite designed to isolate when persistent state is beneficial; it integrates with standard Transformer training pipelines and can be attached to or removed from existing blocks without other architectural changes.

</details>


### [14] [Structure over Pixels: Learning Variable-Length Visual Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27696)
*Piotr Wyrwiński,Kacper Dobek,Krzysztof Krawiec*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: STROP是一种离散视觉分词器架构，能够学习场景的结构化表示，并通过一个专用的长度头在单次前向传播中自适应地估计每个图像的最优编码长度，而无需像素级重建监督。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的自适应分词器要么需要事后搜索，要么在预训练的离散码率集合中选择，无法学习与模型和场景耦合的连续逐图像序列长度，且通常以像素重建为目标，强调纹理而非结构。

Method: 提出STROP架构，包含一个专门的长度头，利用四阶段课程学习，由针对冻结DINOv3特征的局部率失真探针监督，在单次前向传播中估计主动前缀长度；通过绕过像素级重建梯度，完全由高层潜在表示质量塑造码本。

Result: 编码长度随场景复杂度自然增长，在密集预测迁移任务和码本词汇的直接检查中都显现出组合式结构的迹象。

Conclusion: STROP能够学习与场景复杂度匹配的可变长度结构化表示，无需像素级重建，并且能够涌现出组合式结构特性。

Abstract: Discrete visual tokenizers translate images into ordered sequences of codes, providing a natural representation for structural description of scenes. Yet existing adaptive tokenizers either require post-hoc search or select among a discrete set of pre-trained rates, rather than learning a continuous per-image sequence length coupled to the model and scene, and they typically train against pixel reconstruction, emphasizing texture rather than structure. We propose STROP, a discrete visual tokenizer architecture that forms structural scene representations and simultaneously learns how long an image's visual program should be. Using a four-phase curriculum supervised by local rate--distortion probes against frozen DINOv3 features, STROP optimizes a dedicated length head that estimates the active prefix length in a single forward pass. By bypassing pixel-level reconstruction gradients, the codebook is shaped entirely by the quality of higher-level latent representations. Program length grows with scene complexity, and signs of compositional structure emerge both in downstream dense-prediction transfer and in direct inspection of the learned code vocabulary.

</details>


### [15] [Asynchronous Remote Sensing Time-Series Fusion for Cloud Removal and Anytime Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27726)
*Forouzan Fallah,Chia Yu Hsu,Wenwen Li,Anna Liljedahl,Yezhou Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出AGFlow时序对齐生成流匹配模型，融合异步S1 SAR与S2光学数据，实现云去除、时序重建及任意时间点查询，在RESTORE-DiT基准上全缺失帧重建MAE/RMSE降低16-19%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 频繁云层严重限制Sentinel-2光学时序数据的地表监测应用，而Sentinel-1 SAR虽可全天候补充观测，但两者采集不规则且异步，现有融合方法依赖外部配准或只能重建观测时刻，难以应对长间隙重建和按需合成需求。

Method: 提出AGFlow模型，包含三部分：(1) 时间戳条件内部对齐，无需预处理配准即可融合异步S1和含云S2观测；(2) 时空上下文感知去噪，联合建模空间结构与时间动态；(3) 任意时间查询，可在监测窗口内生成无云S2帧。基于RESTORE-DiT基准协议进行评估。

Result: 在RESTORE-DiT基准上，全缺失帧重建MAE和RMSE比RESTORE-DiT降低16-19%；在持续间隙下提供可靠重建；云去除性能具有竞争力；支持灵活时间查询用于下游任务如密集植被监测。

Conclusion: AGFlow有效解决了异步S1/S2融合与任意时刻高时间分辨率重建问题，显著提升长间隙重建质量，为地表连续监测提供了灵活实用的方案。

Abstract: Frequent cloud cover severely limits the usability of Sentinel-2 (S2) optical time series for Earth surface monitoring. Sentinel-1 (S1) SAR provides all-weather complementary observations, but practical S1/S2 fusion remains difficult because acquisitions are irregular and asynchronous. Many existing approaches assume temporally aligned inputs (or require external nearest-date matching) and typically restore only observed timestamps, limiting reconstruction under long gaps and preventing on-demand synthesis. We propose AGFlow (Time Aligned Generative Flow Matching), a spatiotemporal flow-matching model for S1/S2 cloud removal and time-series reconstruction with three capabilities: (1) timestamp-conditioned internal alignment that fuses asynchronous S1 and cloudy S2 observations without preprocessing-based pairing; (2) spatiotemporal, context-aware denoising that models spatial structure jointly with temporal dynamics (rather than independent per-pixel time series); and (3) anytime querying, enabling generation of cloud-free S2 frames at both observed and user-specified timestamps within the monitoring window. We evaluate on the RESTORE-DiT benchmark protocol with quantitative metrics, qualitative comparisons, and component ablations. AGFlow notably improves fully missing-frame reconstruction (MAE and RMSE reduce by 16-19% over RESTORE-DiT) and provides reliable reconstructions under persistent gaps, while also yielding competitive cloud removal performance and flexible temporal querying for downstream tasks such as dense vegetation monitoring.

</details>


### [16] [Bounded-Compute Multimodal Regression for Product-Rating Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27737)
*William Leach,Ru He,Sizhuo Ma,Yizhen Jia,Min Cao,Jian Wang,Rick Cao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种SmolVLM2-256M-Video-Instruct的有界计算适配方法，用于产品评分预测。通过用轻量级MLP替换语言建模头、固定输入尺寸等改进，在LoViF 2026竞赛中实现了0.39 PLCC和0.40 CES的基线性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型(VLM)在多模态质量评估中日益流行，但其默认的自回归文本生成和动态视觉处理方式在严格的延迟预算下与标量回归任务不匹配，需要更高效的适配方案。

Method: 1) 用轻量级两层MLP替代语言建模头，输入为池化的解码器状态；2) 固定图像尺寸为384x384并截断元数据，确保确定性输入；3) 采用静态全局图像处理而非动态分块；4) 将训练数据从100K扩展到1600万样本。

Result: 在官方保留集评估中，228M参数的模型达到0.39 PLCC和0.40 CES；静态全局图像处理略优于动态分块；扩大训练数据显著提升验证相关性。

Conclusion: 该工作为资源受限环境下的多模态回归任务提供了一个强且可复现的基线，验证了特征回归方法在效率上优于基于token的评分生成。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) are increasingly attractive for multimodal quality assessment, but their default reliance on autoregressive text generation and dynamic visual processing is poorly matched to scalar regression under strict latency budgets. We present a bounded-compute adaptation of SmolVLM2-256M-Video-Instruct for product-rating prediction in the LoViF 2026 Efficient VLM challenge. Motivated by recent multimodal engagement-prediction results showing that feature-based regression can outperform token-based score generation, we replace the language-modeling head with a lightweight two-layer MLP fed by pooled decoder states, and we enforce deterministic inputs through fixed 384x384 images and truncated metadata. Across controlled ablations, static global image processing slightly outperforms dynamic tiling, and scaling from 100K to 16M training examples substantially improves validation correlation. Under the official held-out evaluation, our 228M-parameter model achieves 0.39 PLCC and 0.40 CES, providing a strong and reproducible baseline for resource-constrained multimodal regression.

</details>


### [17] [Mahalanobis PatchCore: Covariance-Aware and Streaming-Compatible Industrial Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27748)
*Niccolò Ferrari,Oligert Osmani,Evelina Lamma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Mahalanobis PatchCore，一种协方差感知、流式兼容的工业视觉异常检测方法，通过马氏距离替代欧氏距离进行检索，并采用流式训练管道降低峰值内存，在保持检测性能的同时将内存从5.41GB降至2.78GB。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准PatchCore使用欧氏距离忽略了特征相关性，且其离线构建方式需要先存储所有正常图像块特征后再进行子采样，导致内存开销大，不适合实际工业检测中的内存限制环境。

Method: 在降维后的特征空间中估计正则化协方差模型并对嵌入进行白化处理，使得白化后的欧氏距离等价于马氏距离；采用增量式降维、在线协方差估计和流式聚合构建内存库，无需一次性存储所有正常图像块。

Result: 在15类公开工业异常检测基准上保持与离线PatchCore相近的图像级性能，峰值内存从5.41GB降至2.78GB；在三个工业数据集上平均ROC-AUC从0.981提升至0.986。

Conclusion: Mahalanobis PatchCore在工业异常检测中兼顾了检测精度与内存效率，适用于内存受限的实际产线部署场景。

Abstract: Industrial visual anomaly detection is usually one-class: normal images are abundant, while defects are rare, heterogeneous, and often unavailable during system design. PatchCore-style retrieval suits this setting because it scores test images from a memory bank of normal patch features, but the standard Euclidean geometry ignores feature correlations and its offline construction materialises the full patch pool before subsampling.
  We introduce Mahalanobis PatchCore, a covariance-aware, streaming-compatible extension of PatchCore. Its artificial intelligence contribution is a retrieval detector that estimates a regularised covariance model in reduced feature space and whitens embeddings, so Euclidean nearest-neighbour search after transformation implements Mahalanobis retrieval. A bounded-memory, re-iterable training pipeline builds the memory bank without storing all normal patches at once, using incremental dimensionality reduction, online covariance estimation, and streaming aggregation.
  The engineering application is automated industrial inspection, where visual anomaly detection must remain accurate under practical memory limits. We evaluate the method on a public 15-category industrial anomaly-detection benchmark and three industrial datasets covering blow-fill-seal strip-ampoule meniscus inspection, amber-glass-ampoule bottom inspection, and lyophilised-cake vial inspection. Mahalanobis PatchCore preserves most offline PatchCore image-level performance on the public benchmark while reducing peak memory from 5.41 to 2.78 GB, and improves the selected industrial mean image area under the receiver operating characteristic curve from 0.981 to 0.986.

</details>


### [18] [AndroidDaily: A Verifiable Benchmark for Mobile GUI Agents on Real-World Closed-Source Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27761)
*Yifan Sui,Xin Huang,Hongbing Li,Fang Xu,Jiahe Lv,Haolong Yan,Yeqing Shen,Litao Liu,Zhimin Fan,Ziyang Meng,Jia Wang,Junbo Qi,Kaijun Tan,Zheng Ge,Xiangyu Zhang,Daxin Jiang,Osamu Yoshie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了AndroidDaily基准（含350个真实任务、94个高频Android应用）和GRADE评估方法（基于三层外部可观察指南的自动诊断评估器），在不依赖内部状态的情况下实现自动评估，最佳模型成功率仅62%，揭示当前移动GUI agent与现实需求之间的显著差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GUI基础模型和移动智能体评估多基于模拟环境或开源应用，无法评估真实世界中的闭源应用，因为闭源应用不暴露内部状态，传统自动验证方法失效。

Method: 构建AndroidDaily基准（350个日常任务、94个高频应用），提出GRADE评估器，基于三层可观察外部指南（操作义务、输出质量、负面约束）追踪智能体的视觉轨迹，进行步骤级诊断判断，实现无需内部状态的可验证评估。

Result: GRADE与人工评估者的一致性达到87.37%；最强模型在AndroidDaily上成功率为62.0%，表明当前推理能力与实际移动工作流执行之间仍存在显著差距。

Conclusion: GRADE框架有效解决了闭源应用环境下移动智能体的自动评估难题，揭示了当前模型在真实移动场景中的能力瓶颈，为未来研究指明了方向。

Abstract: The rapid development of GUI foundation models and mobile GUI agents has spurred numerous evaluation benchmarks, yet most rely on simulated environments or open-source applications, leaving real-world closed-source applications largely unevaluated. The core difficulty is that closed-source applications do not expose internal states, making traditional automatic verification inapplicable. To bridge this gap, we introduce AndroidDaily, a large-scale benchmark comprising 350 realistic daily-use tasks across 94 high-frequency Android applications spanning transportation, shopping, local services, entertainment, content creation, social media, and everyday utilities. To enable automatic and verifiable assessment in these opaque environments, we propose Guideline-grounded Reviewer for Automatic Diagnostic Evaluation (GRADE), a process-aware evaluator built on a three-tiered system of observable external guidelines: operational obligations, output quality, and negative constraints. GRADE tracks the agent's visual trajectory against these criteria and produces step-level diagnostic judgments, turning long-horizon, open-ended mobile interactions into verifiable evaluation without relying on hidden internal states. Experiments show that GRADE achieves 87.37\% agreement with human evaluators. The strongest model reaches a 62.0\% success rate on AndroidDaily, highlighting a substantial gap between current reasoning capabilities and practical execution in realistic mobile workflows.

</details>


### [19] [Can Segmentation Models Understand the World? Towards Proactive Affordance Reasoning via Visual Chain-of-Thought](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27764)
*Yuchen Guo,Junli Gong,Hongmin Cai,Yiu-ming Cheung,Weifeng Su*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SegWorld提出一种基于多级视觉思维链（CoT）的意图级分割模型，在接收指令前主动观察场景进行推理，从而将高层意图映射到可操作的物体部位掩码，在意图级指令上显著优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分割模型依赖目标指向式指令（描述、约束或暗示待分割区域），但在真实世界具身交互中，人类指令常处于意图层面（描述期望结果而不指明具体区域），现有模型无法处理这种语义鸿沟。

Method: 构建多级视觉思维链：①在接收指令前主动观察场景，描述可见物体并推断其支持的可能事件；②给定指令后，继续推理链条：从与意图相关的物体 → 满足意图的动作 → 物理交互部位（即支撑该动作的物体部件）。将整个过程形式化为概率推断，主动观察提供语言场景上下文以改进掩码预测。

Result: 在目标指向式指令上匹配基线方法，在意图级指令上大幅提升性能。

Conclusion: 多级视觉思维链能有效弥合意图级指令与目标级分割之间的鸿沟，主动场景推理为具身交互中的高层指令理解提供了可行范式。

Abstract: Recent segmentation models couple large language models (LLMs) with mask decoders to ground complex language expressions into masks, yet their instructions remain target-referential: they describe, constrain, or imply the region to be segmented. However, in real-world embodied interaction, human instructions are often at the intent-level, which includes the desired outcome without naming the region that enables it. To bridge this gap, we introduce SegWorld, where the model reasons about the scene through a multi-level visual chain-of-thought (CoT) before committing to a mask. Before receiving any instructions, it proactively observes the scene, describing visible objects and inferring plausible events they may support. Given an instruction, it continues the chain: from the object relevant to the intent, through the action that satisfies it, to the physical interaction site, the object part that affords the action. We formalize SegWorld as probabilistic inference, in which proactive observation supplies a linguistic scene context that improves mask prediction when instructions are given at the level of intent. We construct an intent-to-part benchmark for evaluating affordance-bearing part segmentation from high-level goals. Experiments show SegWorld matches instruction-driven baselines on target-referential instructions and improves substantially on intent-level ones.

</details>


### [20] [CuriosAI Submission to the CASTLE Challenge at EgoVis 2026](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27800)
*Yuto Kanda,Hayato Tanoue,Takayuki Hori*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CASTLE 2026是一个基于600+小时多视角自我中心视频的185道多选题基准。本文提出两种方法：SVA（搜索-验证-回答）三阶段流水线在四种反事实幻觉规则下达到0.50准确率；TMKG（时间多模态知识图谱）方法达到0.35准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对多视角自我中心视频中的时间多模态问答任务，探索有效的方法来整合长时间跨度的多模态证据。

Method: 在共享的多模态预处理层（包含个人时间线、说话人分辨文本、多VLM字幕集成）基础上，提出两种方法：SVA（分层搜索主窗口→VLM验证子窗口→LLM证据融合）和TMKG（构建时间多模态知识图谱→图搜索定位主单元格→单VLM推理回答）。

Result: SVA在排行榜上达到0.50准确率，TMKG达到0.35准确率。SVA被选为最终竞赛提交方案。

Conclusion: SVA的三阶段分层流水线结合反事实幻觉规则和证据优先级机制在多视角自我中心视频问答任务上优于TMKG的时间知识图谱方法。

Abstract: CASTLE 2026 asks 185 multiple-choice questions over 600+ hours of synchronized multi-view egocentric video. We explore two approaches on top of a shared multimodal preprocessing layer, including per-person timelines, speaker-resolved transcripts, and multi-VLM caption ensembles. Approach A, SVA: Search-Verify-Answer, is a three-stage pipeline that hierarchically narrows to a primary window, verifies sub-windows with a VLM under four anti-confabulation rules, and fuses evidence with an LLM judge under an evidence-priority hierarchy. Approach B, TMKG: Temporal-Multimodal-Knowledge-Graph, is the contrast: it builds a temporal multimodal knowledge graph, locates a primary cell via graph search, and produces the final answer with a single grounded VLM. SVA reaches a leaderboard accuracy of 0.50 and is our final challenge submission; TMKG reaches 0.35.

</details>


### [21] [Residualized Temporal Sparse Autoencoders for Interpreting Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27813)
*Calvin Yeung,Prathyush Poduval,Ali Zakeri,Zhuowen Zou,Mohsen Imani*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出残差化时序稀疏自编码器（Residualized Temporal SAE），通过对扩散模型去噪过程中的激活轨迹进行残差化处理，学习超越线性可预测结构的稀疏潜在特征，用于分析扩散模型内部神经层的时空激活模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型内部神经层的激活呈现轨迹而非静态表示，现有SAE方法仅分析单个时间步或简单以时间为条件，无法有效捕捉激活轨迹中超越线性动态的结构化信息。

Method: 收集去噪过程中各时间步的激活，拟合相邻时间步之间的线性预测器，用初始激活加上未被线性动态解释的残差分量表示每条轨迹；对该残差化表示训练SAE，并将解码器方向映射回原始激活空间以获得每个潜在特征的去噪时间轨迹。

Result: 通过重构与消融研究、时空特征分析以及对Stable Diffusion 1.5的定性引导实验，验证了该方法捕捉时空结构化扩散激活的有效性。

Conclusion: 残差化时序SAE为研究扩散模型中具有时间结构的激活提供了有效框架。

Abstract: Text-to-image diffusion models generate images through an iterative denoising process, so internal neural layers produce trajectories of activations rather than single static representations. Sparse autoencoders (SAEs) have recently been used to decompose diffusion activations into interpretable feature directions, but most approaches analyze activations at individual timesteps or condition on time rather than learning directly from full activation trajectories. In this work, we introduce residualized temporal SAEs for diffusion activation trajectories. We collect activations across denoising time, fit linear predictors between neighboring timesteps, and represent each trajectory using an initial activation together with residual components not explained by these linear dynamics. Training an SAE on this residualized representation encourages sparse latents to capture structure beyond what is linearly predictable. The residualized decoder directions can be mapped back into activation space, allowing each latent to be analyzed as a feature trajectory over denoising time. Through reconstruction and ablation studies, spatiotemporal feature analysis, and qualitative steering experiments on Stable Diffusion~1.5, we show that residualized temporal SAEs provide a useful framework for studying temporally structured diffusion activations.

</details>


### [22] [Pattern Recognition Tasks with Personalized Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27816)
*Md. Arifur Rahman,Isha Das,Mushfiqur Rahman Abir,B. M. Taslimul Haque,Abdullah Al Noman,Abir Ahmed,Md. Jakir Hossen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文对七种个性化联邦学习(PFL)算法在三个数据集上进行综合比较分析，发现APPLE、FedGC和FedProto在模式识别任务中表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的联邦学习方法难以适应客户端异构数据分布，而PFL通过个性化模型更新在保护隐私的同时提升准确性和定制化能力，亟需系统性比较研究来指导算法选择。

Method: 对七种不同的PFL算法在MNIST、SignMNIST和Digit5三个数据集上进行实证比较，采用准确率、精确率、召回率和F1分数作为评估指标，同时深入分析各算法的工作流程、优势与局限性。

Result: APPLE、FedGC和FedProto三种算法在所有评估数据集上持续展现出优越性能，成为稳健的候选算法；其他算法则表现出上下文特异性，需要通过迭代优化才能达到最佳效果。

Conclusion: 个性化联邦学习在异构数据场景下显著优于传统联邦学习，但算法选择需结合具体任务和数据特征；APPLE、FedGC和FedProto是当前模式识别任务中的首选方案。

Abstract: Personalized Federated Learning (PFL) constitutes a novel paradigm that tailors Machine Learning (ML) models to individual clients, thereby furnishing personalized model updates whilst upholding stringent data privacy principles. Diverging from conventional standard Federated Learning (FL) approaches, PFL adapts models to distinct client data distributions, engendering heightened levels of accuracy, customization, and data security, all while minimizing communication overhead. This methodology proves particularly salient in contexts marked by pattern recognition tasks reliant upon heterogeneous data sources and underpinned by paramount privacy apprehensions. In the present research endeavor, this article undertake a comprehensive comparative analysis of seven distinct PFL algorithms deployed across three diverse datasets, namely MNIST, SignMNIST, and Digit5. The overarching objective entails ascertaining the preeminent PFL algorithm, within the framework of pattern recognition tasks, through a rigorous evaluation anchored in metrics encompassing Accuracy, Precision, Recall, and F1 Score. Concurrently, an in-depth scrutiny of these PFL algorithms is conducted, elucidating their operative workflows, advantages, and limitations. Through empirical investigation, the findings evince that APPLE, FedGC, and FedProto emerge as stalwart contenders, consistently furnishing superior performance across the spectrum of assessed datasets, while acknowledging the contextual specificity of alternative algorithms and the potential for iterative refinement to realize optimal outcomes.

</details>


### [23] [A self-supervised learning approach to deep filter banks for texture recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27843)
*Joao B. Florindo,Lucas O. Lyra,Antonio E. Fabris*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于卷积自编码器与深度滤波器结合Fisher向量池化的自监督预训练框架，用于小样本纹理识别，在保持较低计算复杂度的同时提升了分类精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纹理识别中训练数据有限，而现有自监督方法（如掩码自编码器）依赖计算密集的Vision Transformer架构。纹理图像的关键信息集中在每个像素周围的局部区域，无需长距离依赖建模，因此需要一种更高效的预训练方案。

Method: 采用卷积自编码器作为预训练模型，替代计算量大的Vision Transformer；在编码器中引入深度滤波器，并结合Fisher向量池化层，以更好地捕捉纹理模式中的丰富信息。

Result: 在多个纹理数据库上与多种SOTA方法对比，该方法在分类精度和计算复杂度两方面均展现出优势。

Conclusion: 针对纹理图像局部信息密集的特性，卷积自编码器配合深度滤波与Fisher向量池化的自监督预训练框架能够有效提升小样本纹理识别性能，且无需增加显著计算负担。

Abstract: An important challenge in texture recognition is the limited amount of data for training frequently found in real-world applications. In computer vision in general, a successful strategy to mitigate this issue is the use of a pretraining stage where the neural network learns to identify relations between parts of the data in a self-supervised manner. A well-established framework in this direction is masked autoencoder. Nevertheless, these models usually rely on computationally intensive architectures, such as vision transformers. In the particular case of texture images, most of the relevant information is compacted within a delimited area around each pixel, which suggests that capturing long-range dependence via the attention mechanism may be unnecessary. Based on that assumption, here we propose a framework where the pretraining model is a convolutional autoencoder. To leverage the rich information conveyed by texture patterns, we employ deep filters coupled with Fisher vector pooling. In this way, we improve the performance of texture recognition without adding significant computational burden. Our approach is compared with several state-of-the-art methods in different texture databases, confirming its potential both in terms of classification accuracy and computational complexity.

</details>


### [24] [Reflective Dialogue between Teacher and Solver Agents for Video Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27885)
*Takuya Murakawa,Toru Tamaki*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无需微调、仅通过推理阶段上下文注入来适配视频问答任务的方法。该方法构建了一种"反思性对话"——两个智能体之间的多轮对话，教师提出问题并给出正确性反馈，学生作答并提供视觉基础解释（反思），该对话历史作为推理阶段的上下文。在EgoCross基准上超越了零样本和标准上下文学习方法，在CVPR 2026 EgoVis Workshop的跨域挑战中获开源赛道第三名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频问答方法（如微调和上下文学习）难以在不进行微调的情况下，仅从少量标注支持集中获取任务特定知识并应用于推理阶段。

Method: 提出Reflective Dialogue (RD)方法：构建两个智能体（Teacher和Solver）之间的多轮对话，Teacher提出支持问题并给出正确性反馈，Solver作答并提供视觉基础解释（包括正确和错误答案的反思），将该对话历史作为推理阶段的上下文注入。

Result: 在EgoCross基准上，该方法优于零样本基线和标准上下文学习方法，在CVPR 2026 EgoVis Workshop首届跨域EgoCross挑战赛开源赛道中获得第三名。

Conclusion: 仅通过推理阶段的上下文注入即可有效实现VLMs在视频问答领域的跨领域适配，无需微调，且反思性对话结构比直接传递支持集示例更有效。

Abstract: Various approaches have been proposed to adapt Vision-Language Models (VLMs) to specialized domains for Video Question Answering, including fine-tuning and in-context learning. However, acquiring task-specific knowledge at the inference phase from only a small labeled support set without fine-tuning remains a challenge. In this paper, we propose a method that achieves adaptation solely through inference-time context injection. Our method first constructs a Reflective Dialogue (RD) -- a multi-turn conversation between two agents, in which Teacher poses each support question and delivers correctness feedback, and Solver answers and provides visual grounding explanations (or reflections) for both correct and incorrect answers. This dialogue history is then used as context at the inference phase. Experiments on the EgoCross benchmark demonstrate that our method outperforms both a baseline zero-shot setting and a standard in-context learning approach that passes support set examples directly, achieving 3rd place in the Open-source Track of the 1st Cross-Domain EgoCross Challenge at the CVPR 2026 EgoVis Workshop, for which this paper also serves as a technical report.

</details>


### [25] [SmartDirector: Keyframe-Conditioned Cinematic Video Generation with Narrative Pacing Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27891)
*Zhida Zhang,Jie Ma,Zhan Peng,Haoxue Wu,Yang Han,Jun Liang,Jie Cao,Jing Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SmartDirector 是一个通过多关键帧增强视频生成叙事能力的框架，包含 Director-Gen（低分辨率生成）和 Director-SR（高分辨率细化）两阶段，支持单镜头生成、多镜头叙事合成和视频扩展，在多项实验中优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频生成方法依赖稀疏条件信号（如文本提示或首尾帧），对叙事结构和时间节奏的控制能力有限，难以生成具有高质量叙事内容的视频。

Method: 提出 SmartDirector 框架，包含两阶段：Director-Gen 基于多关键帧生成低分辨率视频；Director-SR 利用高分辨率关键帧作为语义锚点恢复细节。同时构建了从电影中提取单镜头和多镜头序列的数据管道以支持训练。

Result: 实验表明 SmartDirector 在多项指标上显著优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: 多关键帧条件能够有效提升视频生成模型的叙事控制能力，代码将开源以促进后续研究。

Abstract: The narrative quality of a video fundamentally determines its perceptual value. Although existing video generation methods can produce visually appealing content, they predominantly rely on sparse conditioning signals such as text prompts or first/last frames, which limits precise control over narrative structure and temporal pacing. In this paper, we propose SmartDirector, a framework that enhances the narrative capacity of video generation models through multiple keyframes. SmartDirector supports flexible generation scenarios including single-shot generation, multi-shot narrative synthesis, and video extension. The framework operates in two stages: Director-Gen generates a low-resolution video conditioned on the provided keyframes, and Director-SR refines the output by exploiting high-resolution keyframes as semantic anchors to recover fine-grained details. To enable robust multi-keyframe training, we construct a data pipeline that curates single-shot and multi-shot sequences from movies. Extensive experiments demonstrate that SmartDirector substantially outperforms existing state-of-the-art approaches. We will release the code to facilitate further research.

</details>


### [26] [Towards Unified Vision-Language Models with Incomplete Multi-Modal Inputs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27894)
*Xiang Fang,Wanlong Fang,Changshuo Wang,Keke Tang,Daizong Liu,Siyi Wang,Wei Ji*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文首次提出统一的非完整视频-语言模型，用于处理传感器失效（如摄像头因隐私问题关闭）导致的模态缺失数据，可作为即插即用模块提升现有VLM在多模态任务中的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频-语言模型（VLM）假设输入数据完整，但真实场景中传感器可能因隐私等原因失效，导致模态不完整数据。这种训练-测试不一致性对VLM的安全性、可信度的影响被长期忽视。

Method: 提出一种统一的非完整视频-语言模型，能够处理模态缺失的多模态输入，设计为即插即用模块以兼容现有VLM架构。

Result: 大量实验表明，该方法作为即插即用模块能有效提升现有VLM在多种多模态任务上的性能。

Conclusion: 该工作是首个系统研究VLM在模态不完整输入下安全性与可信度的工作，所提方法具备良好的通用性和实用性。

Abstract: Video-Language Models (VLMs) have demonstrated impressive multi-modal reasoning capabilities across diverse computer vision applications. However, these VLMs are task-specific and assume that both video and language inputs are complete. However, real-world VLM applications might face challenges due to deactivated sensors (e.g., cameras are unavailable due to data privacy), yielding modality-incomplete data and leading to inconsistency between training and testing data. While straightforward incomplete input can boast training generalization-ability and lead to training failure, its potential risks to VLMs regarding safety and trustworthiness have been largely neglected. To this end, we make the first attempt to propose a unified incomplete video-language model to process the incomplete multi-modal inputs. Extensive experimental results show that our method can serve as a plug-and-play module for previous works to improve their performance in various multi-modal tasks.

</details>


### [27] [Decoupled Training with Local Reinforcement Fine-Tuning in Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27900)
*Yuting Ma,Lechao Cheng,Xiaohua Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出FedDTL框架，通过解耦图像编码器和文本编码器、服务器-客户端模态对齐以及两阶段本地微调（监督微调+强化学习），在异构和全数据联邦学习场景下平衡视觉-语言模型的全局任务适应性与泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联邦视觉-语言模型方法在异构和全数据FL设置下，过分强调本地优化和简单参数聚合，导致客户端间优化不一致和客户端内过专门化问题，难以平衡全局任务适应性与泛化能力。

Method: 1）解耦图像编码器和文本编码器进行跨客户端和服务器训练；2）服务器-客户端模态对齐促进全局语义一致性更新；3）两阶段本地微调：监督微调阶段实现快速可靠的热启动，强化学习阶段增强泛化。

Result: 在多个基准测试（包括标签偏移和特征偏移）上，FedDTL在少样本和全数据场景下均能有效平衡全局任务适应性与泛化能力。

Conclusion: FedDTL通过解耦编码器训练和两阶段微调策略，有效解决了联邦VLM在异构数据下的优化不一致和过专门化问题，实现了全局适应性与泛化的平衡。

Abstract: Federated Learning (FL) with pre-trained Vision-Language Models (VLMs) has emerged as a promising paradigm for various downstream tasks. By leveraging its strong representations, recent studies improve task adaptation under insufficient local data while preserving generalization. However, these methods emphasize fully local optimization with simple parameter aggregation,which can amplify inter-client optimization inconsistency and intra-client over-specialization under heterogeneous and full-data FL settings, making it difficult to balance global task adaptation and generalization. To address these challenges, we propose FedDTL, a novel federated VLM framework that decouples the image encoder and text encoder across clients and the server. Through decoupled encoder training with server-client modality alignment, FedDTL promotes coherent global semantic update and reduces inter-client optimization inconsistency, improving global task adaptation.To further mitigate intra-client over-specialization,we introduce a two-stage local fine-tuning, where a supervised fine-tuning stage enables rapid and reliable warm-start, followed by a reinforcement learning stage that enhances generalization. Extensive experiments on multiple benchmarks, including label skew and feature shift, demonstrate that FedDTL achieves an effective balance between global task adaptation and generalization under various FL data distributions in both few-shot and full-data regimes.

</details>


### [28] [Rethinking Video-Language Model from the Language Input Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27920)
*Xiang Fang,Wanlong Fang,Changshuo Wang,Xiaoye Qu,Daizong Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种即插即用框架，通过生成正负文本、属性推理和跨模态桥接，解决视频语言模型中文本模板预定义的局限性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM方法隐式假设所有文本由特定模板预定义，但实际应用中这种假设不成立：预定义所有文本耗时耗力，且模板化输入限制过多、用户不友好。此外，语义相似但模板不同的文本会导致性能差异。

Method: 1) 从原始文本生成正负文本，以定位特定文本组件；2) 提出基于属性的文本推理策略，挖掘生成文本的细粒度语义；3) 以视频为指导，设计自加权损失函数进行跨模态桥接。

Result: 该方法作为即插即用模块，能有效提升现有最先进VLM的性能。

Conclusion: 所提出的即插即用框架可以灵活适配多种VLM方法，通过细粒度文本语义挖掘和视频引导的跨模态对齐，克服了固定模板的局限性。

Abstract: Driven by the wave of large language models, Video-Language Models (VLMs) have become a significant yet challenging technology to bridge the gap between videos and texts. Although previous VLM works have made significant progress, almost all of them implicitly assume that all the texts are predefined by the specific template. In real-world applications, such a strict assumption is impossible to satisfy since 1) predefining all the texts is extremely time-consuming and labor-intensive. 2) these predefined text inputs are too restrictive and user-unfriendly, limiting their applications. It is observed that given a video input, texts with similar semantics but different templates lead to various performances. To this end, in this paper, we propose a novel plug-and-play framework for various VLM-based methods to fully bridge videos and texts. Specifically, we first generate positive and negative texts from the original ones to target specific text components. Then, we propose an attribute-based text reasoning strategy to mine fine-grained textual semantics of generated texts. Finally, we utilize videos as guidance to conduct cross-modal bridging by designing a self-weighted loss. Extensive experiments show that the proposed method can serve as the plug-and-play module to effectively improve the performance of state-of-the-art VLMs.

</details>


### [29] [SIGMA: Semantic-Difference Instruction-Grounding Mask Annotator for Text-Driven Image Manipulation Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27924)
*Peiyu Zhuang,Jianquan Yang,Haodong Li,Zhuoying Cai,Ruitao Xie,Jishen Zeng,Baoying Chen,Jiwu Huang,Xiaochun Cao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SIGMA，一种从公开编辑数据中自动生成像素级篡改定位掩码的方法，无需人工标注，即可大规模构建图像篡改定位(IML)训练数据集。SIGMA通过视觉基础模型中的语义特征差异分析，结合指令引导的空间先验，在5个基准上超越现有自动掩码生成方法，并生成约110万规模的IML训练集，显著提升6种检测器的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图像篡改定位(IML)模型需要大量像素级标注的训练数据，但获取此类数据的成本高昂。现有公开编辑数据集中包含数百万对(原始图,编辑后图)结构，与IML训练样本形式一致，唯独缺少像素级掩码。若能低成本自动恢复这些掩码，即可将海量现有编辑数据转化为IML监督资源。

Method: SIGMA包含两个阶段训练：Stage I用修复掩码进行监督预训练；Stage II通过VAE往返噪声校准、EMA自训练和编辑-噪声解耦损失来弥合扩散域偏移。核心机制是在视觉基础模型中进行语义特征差异分析，并通过双向跨模态精炼将指令引导的空间先验注入视觉流，在忠实实现用户意图的编辑区域放大差异信号。

Result: 在5个基准上，SIGMA相比现有自动掩码生成方法，F1提升12.20%，IoU提升11.16%。利用SIGMA从公开编辑语料库生成约110万IML训练集，使6种不同检测器在5个数据集上的F1平均提升18.34%。

Conclusion: SIGMA成功将海量公开编辑数据转化为模型无关的IML监督资源，证明了无需人工标注即可大规模构建高质量IML训练数据的可行性，为图像编辑定位领域提供了低成本的规模化数据解决方案。

Abstract: Text-driven image editing has advanced rapidly, but reliably localizing these manipulations requires image manipulation localization (IML) models trained on large pixel-annotated datasets, and there is still no low-cost way to obtain such training data at scale. We observe that these data already exist in disguise: public editing datasets contain millions of structurally identical (original, edited) pairs to IML training samples, lacking only pixel-level masks. Recovering these masks automatically is non-trivial: pixel differencing is overwhelmed by diffusion-induced perturbations across all pixels, and instruction-only grounding localizes only what the prompt describes, missing unintended editor side-effects. We propose SIGMA (Semantic-difference Instruction-Grounding Mask Annotator), which performs semantic-feature differencing in a vision foundation backbone and injects an instruction-derived spatial prior into this visual stream via bidirectional cross-modal refinement, amplifying the difference signal at intended-edit regions when the editor faithfully realizes user intent. SIGMA is trained in two complementary stages: Stage I supervises on inpainting masks; Stage II closes the diffusion-domain shift via VAE-roundtrip noise calibration, EMA self-training, and an edit-noise disentanglement loss. SIGMA outperforms existing automatic mask generators on five benchmarks (+12.20% F1, +11.16% IoU). When applied to public editing corpora, it produces a ~1.1M IML training set that improves six diverse detectors by +18.34% F1 across five datasets, turning previously unused editing data into a model-agnostic supervisory resource for IML. We'll release the full codebase as soon as the paper is accepted.

</details>


### [30] [Structure-Guided Visual Perturbation Neutralization for LVLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27927)
*Yuanhe Zhang,Xueting Wang,YanBin Ren,Haoran Gao,Xinhan Zheng,Zhenhong Zhou,Fanyu Meng,Li Sun,Sen Su*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SIGN框架，一种轻量级即插即用的防御方法，通过先验结构提取和动态引导中和机制，以仅0.5%像素修改和0.16秒每图的速度实现87%以上的防御成功率，有效抵御针对LVLM的像素级对抗攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有防御大多针对传统计算机视觉设计，忽略了LVLM所需的跨模态对齐，导致性能下降；而针对LVLM的少量防御又需要大量图像修改和计算开销，影响推理质量和效率。

Method: 提出结构引导的中和（SIGN）框架，包含两个核心模块：1) 先验结构提取（Prior Structural Extraction），利用视觉编码器提取图像的结构先验；2) 动态引导中和（Dynamic Guided Neutralization），基于提取的结构信息自适应地抑制对抗扰动，实现高效防御。

Result: 防御成功率超过87%，仅需0.5%像素修改和每张图像0.16秒的处理时间，同时几乎保持原始视觉表示和良性任务性能不变。

Conclusion: SIGN提供了一种轻量级替代方案，无需昂贵的模型训练，展示了利用视觉编码器实现高效对抗防护的潜力。

Abstract: Image inputs enable Large Vision Language Models (LVLMs) to perceive fine-grained visual information, but also introduce a pixel-level attack surface through which adversarial perturbations can elicit unsafe model behaviors. However, most existing defenses are designed for traditional computer vision settings and thus often overlook the cross-modal alignment required by LVLMs, leading to degraded performance. Meanwhile, the limited defenses tailored to LVLMs often require substantial image modifications and introduce considerable computational overhead, thereby compromising inference quality and efficiency. To address these limitations, we propose Structure-Induced Guided Neutralization (SIGN), a lightweight, plug-and-play defense framework that improves LVLM compatibility via Prior Structural Extraction and achieves efficient perturbation suppression via Dynamic Guided Neutralization. Extensive experiments show that SIGN achieves over 87\% defense success rate with only 0.5\% pixel modification and 0.16 seconds per image, while nearly preserving original visual representations and benign task performance. Our work offers a lightweight alternative to defenses that require costly model training and highlights the potential of exploiting a vision encoder for efficient adversarial protection. Our code is open source on https://anonymous.4open.science/r/SIGN-BCB1.

</details>


### [31] [When Think-with-Image Meets Safety: What Determines Multimodal Jailbreak Robustness?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27932)
*Yuan Tian,Bing Hu,Fang Wu,Xiaomin Li,Binghang Lu,Neil Zhenqiang Gong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了多模态大语言模型中"先思考再生成图像"推理范式的安全性，发现显式图像-工具交互能将越狱攻击成功率平均降低约30%，并提出了图像-工具安全向量框架来解释这一现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大视觉语言模型开始采用"先思考再生成图像"的推理范式，但其安全性影响尚不清楚。作者希望系统评估不同推理范式对多模态越狱鲁棒性的影响，并解释其背后的机制。

Method: 在多个视觉语言模型上比较了四种推理范式：直接响应生成、纯文本前序对话、视觉状态操作、显式外部图像工具调用。同时引入了图像-工具安全向量框架，通过隐藏表示中的残差偏移来解释安全性提升，并进行了表示层面分析和激活干预实验。

Result: 显式图像-工具交互在所有评估范式中取得了最低的攻击成功率，平均相对降低约30%。即使在返回图像输出被手动覆盖或本身不安全的情况下，ASR仍然较低；但在纯文本前序控制下ASR回升至接近直接回答的水平。图像-工具安全向量框架的分析支持了安全机制源于隐藏表示向安全相关方向偏移的解释。

Conclusion: 显式图像-工具交互是提升越狱鲁棒性的有前景的设计模式，同时也提示需要对具体管道进行安全性评估。

Abstract: Think-with-image reasoning is emerging as a new inference paradigm for large vision-language models, but its safety implications remain poorly understood. Existing systems already span multiple process designs, including direct response generation, text-only prior turn, visual-state manipulation, and explicit external image-tool invocation. In this paper, we ask which of these evaluated paradigms improves multimodal jailbreak robustness, and why. Across multiple vision-language models, explicit image-tool interaction yields the lowest attack success rates in our experiments, reducing jailbreak success by around 30% relative on average across the evaluated models. This finding is initially surprising: ASR remains low even when the returned image-tool output is manually overridden or itself unsafe-looking, but returns near direct-answering levels under text-only prior turn controls. These results indicate that the lower ASR is not explained by benign returned-image semantics or by the textual image-tool trace alone. To explain the pattern, we introduce an image-tool safety vector framework that models image-tool invocation as a residual shift in hidden representations toward a safety-relevant direction. Representation-level analyses and activation interventions support this account. Overall, our results suggest that explicit image-tool interaction is a promising design pattern for improving jailbreak robustness, while also motivating pipeline-specific safety evaluation.

</details>


### [32] [SEMAGIC: Learning Semantically Consistent Deformable 3D Representations from In-the-Wild Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27938)
*Sky Cen,Wufei Ma,Guofeng Zhang,Alan Yuille,Adam Kortylewski*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SEMAGIC通过将可变形建模作为发现类别级语义对应关系的机制，而非单纯追求重建质量，实现了从单视图野外图像学习语义一致的可变形3D表示，在语义对应基准上显著提升14.7个百分点的PCK@0.1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有从单视图图像重建可变形3D物体的方法虽然能产生视觉上合理的几何形状，但跨实例的对应关系不稳定，在语义对应基准上表现较差。作者发现这些模型未能捕获下游任务所需的语义结构。

Method: SEMAGIC框架包含：(1)规范模板网格和学习的变形场，类似自动编码器从图像特征重建实例几何；(2)特征级一致性损失，对齐规范网格与变形网格之间的语义特征；(3)顶点索引条件变形，保持跨实例的语义对应关系。通过将几何变形与语义对齐显式耦合，使得顶点在跨实例中保持一致的语义含义。

Result: 在SPair-71k基准上，SEMAGIC将可变形模型的语义对应PCK@0.1提高了14.7个百分点，证明可变形模型可以作为有效的语义3D表示。

Conclusion: 通过将可变形建模重新定位为发现语义对应关系的机制，而非简单的几何重建任务，可以同时获得几何合理性和语义一致性，建立可变形模型作为语义3D表示的有效性。

Abstract: Learning deformable 3D object models from single-view in-the-wild images has enabled impressive 3D shape reconstruction without supervision. However, it remains unclear whether these models capture the semantic structure required for downstream tasks. We find that existing deformable reconstruction approaches, despite producing visually plausible geometry, yield unstable correspondences across instances and perform poorly on semantic correspondence benchmarks. We introduce SEMAGIC, a framework for learning semantically consistent deformable 3D representations from single-view in-the-wild images. Rather than treating reconstruction as the end goal, SEMAGIC uses deformable modeling as a mechanism to discover category-level correspondences. Each category is represented by a canonical template mesh and a learned deformation field, functioning similarly to an autoencoder that reconstructs instance geometry from image features, enabling vertices to maintain consistent semantic meaning across instances. Semantic consistency is enforced during training through (i) a feature-level consistency loss aligning semantic features between canonical and deformed meshes, and (ii) vertex-index-conditioned deformation that preserves semantic correspondence across instances. By explicitly coupling geometric deformation with semantic alignment, SEMAGIC produces representations that maintain stable part correspondences across intra-category variation. Experiments demonstrate that SEMAGIC improves semantic correspondence of deformable models by +14.7 PCK@0.1 on SPair-71k, establishing deformable models as effective semantic 3D representations.

</details>


### [33] [Evaluating the Feasibility of Inferring Dietary Behavior Change Receptivity from Egocentric Images of Eating Environment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27950)
*Long Li,Yuning Huang,Heather A. Eicher-Miller,J. Graham Thomas,Fengqing Zhu,Edward Sazonov*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文探索利用可穿戴相机拍摄的自我中心饮食图像，结合CLIP视觉编码器和轻量级Transformer分类器，推断参与者的饮食行为改变接受度（意识、互动能力、动机），初步实验结果显示该方法优于简单基线模型，表明自我中心饮食图像可能包含与行为改变接受度相关的线索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确评估饮食行为改变接受度对设计即时自适应干预措施至关重要，但基于自我报告的评估存在数据稀疏和延迟的问题，限制了其在连续监测中的实际应用。本文旨在探索被动感知（可穿戴相机拍摄的自我中心图像）是否能够帮助解决这一挑战。

Method: 采用可穿戴相机AIM-2在自由生活环境中采集自我中心饮食图像序列，并配对参与者自我报告的行为改变接受度（意识、互动能力、动机）数据。使用迁移学习框架，结合预训练的CLIP视觉编码器和轻量级Transformer分类器，处理饮食图像序列以提取与行为改变接受度相关的语义和时间线索。

Result: 初步实验结果显示，所提出的方法在预测行为改变接受度指标上优于简单基线模型，表明自我中心饮食图像中包含与饮食行为改变接受度相关的线索。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明自我中心饮食图像可能包含可推断饮食行为改变接受度的线索，但需要更大规模、更全面的数据集进行进一步验证。该工作为被动感知技术在饮食行为改变接受度连续监测中的应用提供了初步证据。

Abstract: Accurately assessing dietary behavior change receptivity is essential for designing effective just-in-time adaptive interventions (JITAIs) that promote healthier eating habits. However, self-report-based assessment of behavior change receptivity is sparse and delayed, limiting its practical use in continuous monitoring. To explore whether passive sensing may help address this challenge, this study conducts a pilot investigation of inferring participants' self-reported behavior change receptivity from egocentric eating images collected by a wearable camera. We use pilot data obtained from free-living eating episodes using the Automatic Ingestion Monitor v2 (AIM-2). The data included egocentric image sequences captured during eating and paired with responses to questions assessing specific dimensions of behavior change receptivity (awareness, interaction capability, and motivation). To examine whether visual information contained any relevancy to these responses, we evaluated a transfer-learning-assisted framework that combines a pre-trained Contrastive Language-Image Pre-Training (CLIP) vision encoder with a lightweight transformer classifier. The model processes eating episode image sequences to extract potential semantic and temporal cues related to behavior change receptivity. Preliminary experimental results show promising improvements over simple baseline models for behavior change receptivity indicators. These early findings suggest that egocentric eating episode images may contain cues related to dietary behavior change receptivity, and warrant further investigation with larger and more comprehensive datasets.

</details>


### [34] [ROVER: Routing Object-Centric Visual Evidence for Grounded Multi-Image Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27959)
*Guannan Lv,Ren Nie,Hongjian Dou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ROVER，一种轻量级可学习插件，通过目标中心差分注意力机制和步骤特定token三元组，实现多图像推理中的全局视觉证据路由，在MM-GCoT和VideoEspresso上分别提升答案准确率4.8%/8.6%，且具有良好的跨任务迁移能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM在多图像推理中，基于RoI的方法会削弱整体场景理解和物体间关系，且解码成本随RoI数量扩展；而自适应视觉特征选择需要细粒度监督或复杂启发式规则。需一种高效、轻量的全局视觉证据路由方法。

Method: 提出ROVER插件，在每个目标接地预测时注入步骤特定token三元组，实现：(i) 聚合当前推理上下文；(ii) 通过目标中心差分注意力从图像内提取线索到视觉工作空间；(iii) 在该空间中跨物体和图像路由并整合历史感知证据。集成到Qwen2.5-VL-7B，采用SFT-to-GRPO交替训练流程。

Result: 在MM-GCoT上答案准确率提升4.8%，接地准确率提升14.6%；VideoEspresso上答案准确率提升8.6%。VideoEspresso训练模型展现强迁移性，在多个基准上平均超越基座模型4.7%。

Conclusion: ROVER作为一种轻量级、可学习的全局视觉证据路由插件，能有效克服现有方法在整体场景理解和计算效率上的局限，在多图像推理任务中取得显著提升，并展现出良好的跨任务泛化能力。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have increasingly localized and interleaved visual evidence for deliberative reasoning. Grounding-based approaches typically focus on regions of interest (RoIs) by injecting cropped image patches or RoI-specific features into the reasoning context. However, such designs can weaken holistic scene understanding and inter-object relations, while incurring decoding costs that scale with the number and size of RoIs. Alternatively, adaptive visual feature selection often requires fine-grained supervision or complex heuristics. To address these limitations, we propose ROVER (Routing Object-centric Visual Evidence for grounded multi-image Reasoning), a lightweight, learnable plugin for efficient global visual evidence routing. Upon each object grounding prediction, ROVER injects a step-specific token triplet to synergistically: (i) aggregate the ongoing reasoning context, (ii) distill intra-image cues into a visual working space via object-centric differential attention, and (iii) route and integrate history-aware evidence across objects and images within this space for subsequent reasoning. We integrate ROVER into Qwen2.5-VL-7B and develop an interleaved SFT-to-GRPO training pipeline. Strictly adhering to the original datasets and evaluation protocols, our method achieves the best performance on MM-GCoT (+4.8% answer accuracy, +14.6% grounding accuracy) and VideoEspresso (+8.6% answer accuracy). The VideoEspresso-trained model demonstrates strong transferability, outperforming the base model by +4.7% on average across diverse benchmarks.

</details>


### [35] [Mags-RL: Wearing Multimodal LLMs a Magnifying Glass via Agentic Reinforcement Learning For Complex Scene Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27960)
*Xuanzhao Dong,Wenhui Zhu,Peijie Qiu,Xiwen Chen,Xiaobing Yu,Xin Li,Zhipeng Wang,Shao Tang,Gen Li,Yujian Xiong,Hao Wang,Yanxi Chen,Prayag Tiwari,Yalin Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Mags-RL是一个基于强化学习的框架，为多模态大语言模型配备外部超分辨率"放大镜"智能体，通过两轮推理机制（初始推理→识别感兴趣区域→超分辨率放大→重新验证）和高效课程学习策略，仅需40个训练样本即可在VSR、TallyQA、GQA等数据集上取得优异性能，实现高精度视觉推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型在复杂场景（如高目标密度、复杂背景杂乱）下难以准确理解图像，限制了其推理能力。现有方法依赖额外的边界框等显式视觉线索标注，且产生的低分辨率裁剪会丢失细粒度细节，影响推理准确性。

Method: 提出Mags-RL框架，包含两轮推理机制：(1) 第一轮生成初始推理并自主识别感兴趣区域（无需额外标注）；(2) 第二轮调用超分辨率智能体对感兴趣区域进行裁剪和放大，然后重新审视并验证前期推理以产生最终答案。同时引入课程学习策略实现数据高效的强化学习训练。

Result: 在VSR、TallyQA和GQA子集上的实验表明，Mags-RL的性能优于近期强竞争方法，实现了高质量的推理和精确的视觉定位，且仅需40个训练样本即可达到合理性能。

Conclusion: Mags-RL通过智能体强化学习框架，在不依赖额外标注的情况下，利用超分辨率放大智能体实现细粒度视觉推理，显著提升了MLLM在复杂场景下的视觉理解能力，且训练数据效率极高。

Abstract: Despite their popularity and success, Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) often struggle to interpret images accurately, which limits their reasoning capability in complex scenarios (e.g., high object density and complex background clutter). Prior work mainly addresses this limitation by incorporating explicit visual cues like bounding boxes that require extra annotations. In addition, the resulting low-resolution crops often miss fine-grained details that MLLMs require for accurate reasoning. Therefore, we propose Mags-RL, an Agentic Reinforcement Learning (RL) framework that equips MLLMs with an external super-resolution "magnifying glass" agent for high-resolution fine-grained inspection. Specifically, the model performs two-round reasoning: in the first round, it generates an initial rationale and autonomously identifies regions of interest without relying on additional annotations; in the second round, it invokes a super-resolution agent to crop and upscale those regions, then revisits and verifies its earlier reasoning to produce the final answer. We also introduce a novel curriculum learning strategy that enables data-efficient RL training, needing as few as only 40 training samples to achieve reasonable performance. Experiments on VSR, TallyQA, and GQA subsets show its superior performance against recent strong competing methods, demonstrating high-quality reasoning with precise visual grounding. Code and weights will be released soon.

</details>


### [36] [Bridging the Generalization Gap in Adverse Weather Segmentation: A Training Recipe Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27962)
*Cong Xu,Pu Luo,Yumei Li,Boyou Xue*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对CVPR 2026 UG2+ Workshop Track 2中的五种天气退化场景语义分割任务，发现模型存在严重的验证-测试泛化差距（如SegFormer-B5下降16.1 mIoU）。作者不追求架构创新，而是从预训练SegMAN-S出发，系统研究领域自适应微调、多源数据混合、场景平衡采样和合成退化增强等训练策略，最终实现59.9% mIoU，验证-测试差距仅6.5点，远小于大模型。文章还分析了架构修改、损失函数和模型缩放等负面结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型在天气退化场景下存在严重的验证集到测试集的泛化鸿沟，模型容量本身不足以解决鲁棒性问题，亟需探索训练策略而非架构复杂度来缩小这一差距。

Method: 以预训练SegMAN-S为骨干，系统结合领域自适应微调、多源数据混合、场景平衡采样和合成退化增强四种训练策略，并对比分析多种架构修改、损失函数变体和模型缩放方案。

Result: 官方测试集上达到59.9% mIoU，验证-测试差距仅6.5点（比大模型的一半还低），验证了精心设计的训练策略能有效缓解泛化鸿沟。

Conclusion: 在天气鲁棒语义分割中，训练策略的细致设计比增加模型复杂度更有效；通过系统性实验提供了若干负面结果（架构修改、损失函数变体、模型缩放）作为实用经验。

Abstract: This paper describes our approach for the 8th UG2+ Workshop (CVPR 2026) Track~2, which targets semantic segmentation of outdoor scenes degraded by five weather conditions: blur, darkness, snow, haze, and glare. A central challenge we observe is a severe generalization gap -- models that perform well on the validation set often collapse on the test set. For instance, SegFormer-B5 drops 16.1 mIoU points from validation to test, suggesting that model capacity alone is insufficient for robustness. We investigate whether a carefully designed training recipe, rather than architectural complexity, can address this gap. Starting from a pre-trained SegMAN-S backbone, we systematically study the effects of domain-adaptive fine-tuning, multi-source data mixing, scene-balanced sampling, and synthetic degradation augmentation. Our final system achieves 59.9\% mIoU on the official test set while maintaining a validation-test gap of only 6.5 points -- less than half that of larger models.
