### [109] [VibeSearchBench: Benchmarking Long-horizon Proactive Search in the Wild](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27882)
*Xiaohongshu Inc*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出VibeSearch范式来弥补LLM搜索智能体在基准测试与实际用户体验之间的差距，并构建了包含200个双语任务的VibeSearchBench基准，通过渐进式用户模拟和图匹配评估框架对7个前沿模型进行评测，发现最佳F1仅为30.30，表明当前模型在多轮协作搜索方面仍存在显著不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有搜索基准存在查询过于具体、单轮交互、固定模式评估等问题，无法反映真实搜索场景中用户与智能体通过多轮对话协作明确模糊意图的行为，导致评估结果与实际用户体验之间存在持续差距。

Method: 提出VibeSearch范式并构建VibeSearchBench基准：包含200个手工整理的中英双语任务（20个领域），分为专业版和日常版；每个任务包含用户画像和模式无关的真实知识图谱；采用渐进式披露的用户模拟器和图匹配评估框架；在ReAct框架和OpenClaw智能体工具上对7个前沿模型进行基准测试。

Result: 所有模型在VibeSearch任务上均表现不足，最佳F1分数仅为30.30，凸显出在长上下文推理、主动意图引导和结构化知识构建方面亟需根本性进展。

Conclusion: 现有LLM智能体在多轮协作搜索中能力严重不足，需要从长上下文推理、主动意图挖掘和结构化知识构建三个方向进行根本性突破。

Abstract: LLM-based agents score well on search benchmarks, yet real users consistently find results unsatisfying, revealing a persistent evaluation-experience gap. We attribute this gap to existing benchmarks' reliance on over-specified queries, single-turn interactions, and fixed-schema evaluation, none of which reflect real search behavior where users and agents collaboratively refine vague intent through multi-turn dialogue. We term this paradigm VibeSearch and introduce VibeSearchBench, a benchmark comprising 200 manually curated bilingual (Chinese and English) tasks across 20 domains, split into VibeSearch-Pro (professional) and VibeSearch-Daily (daily-life) subsets. Each task pairs a user persona with a schema-free ground-truth knowledge graph, and is evaluated through a progressive-disclosure user simulator and a graph-matching evaluation framework. We benchmark seven frontier models under both the ReAct framework and the OpenClaw agent harness. Results show that all models remain substantially inadequate for VibeSearch (best F1: 30.30), highlighting the need for fundamental advances in long-context reasoning, proactive intent elicitation, and structured knowledge construction.

</details>


### [110] [FinBoardBench: Benchmarking Dynamic Wealth Management and Strategic Financial Reasoning of LLMs via Board Game Simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27896)
*Xuesi Hu,Peng Wang,Jinpeng Miao,Xilin Tao,Caiwei Li,Yue Ma,Jie He,Qiancheng Zhang,Yuntao Zou,Dagang Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出FinBoardBench，一个基于三种经典金融棋盘游戏（现金流、并购、大富翁）的评估套件，用于测试大语言模型在动态财富管理和金融决策中的能力。实验表明，虽然LLM具备基本的长期规划和投资逻辑，但其强大的静态推理能力无法转化为成功的动态决策，尤其是优先获取资产而忽视流动性，使其易受随机金融危机的冲击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有静态金融基准无法评估LLM在真实环境中动态财富管理和金融决策的能力。

Method: 基于Cashflow、Acquire和Monopoly三款经典金融棋盘游戏构建评估套件FinBoardBench，涵盖个人现金流管理、企业投资并购预测及竞争性谈判拍卖等金融技能，并对9个先进LLM进行实验。

Result: LLM具备基本长期规划和投资逻辑，但在复杂交互中无法有效利用博弈获利；静态推理能力强但无法转化为成功的动态决策；倾向于优先获取资产而忽视流动性，易因随机事件陷入财务危机。

Conclusion: FinBoardBench可为未来更智能的基于LLM的决策系统提供有价值的参考。

Abstract: Recently, large language models (LLMs) have achieved superior performance in static financial reasoning and simple dynamic trading tasks. However, existing static financial benchmarks are insufficient to assess the dynamic wealth management and financial decision-making capabilities of LLMs in real-world environments. To bridge this gap, we present FinBoardBench, an evaluation suite based on three classic financial board games: Cashflow, Acquire, and Monopoly. FinBoardBench assesses a comprehensive set of financial skills, including personal cash flow management with debt balancing, corporate investment and acquisition forecasting, and competitive trade negotiations with asset auctions. Our experiments with 9 advanced LLMs reveal that while exhibiting basic long-term planning and investment logic, they fail to effectively leverage complex interactions for profit, and their strong static reasoning performance does not transform into successful dynamic decision-making. Notably, they tend to prioritize immediate asset acquisition over maintaining sufficient liquidity, making them vulnerable to financial crises triggered by random events. We hope that FinBoardBench can provide a valuable reference for more intelligent LLM-based decision-making systems in the future.

</details>


### [111] [The Fragility of Chain-of-Thought Monitoring Across Typologically Diverse Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27901)
*Eric Onyame,Runtao Zhou,Kowshik Thopalli,Bhavya Kailkhura,Chirag Agarwal*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文首次大规模评估了跨13种语言和7个前沿模型家族的思维链（CoT）可监控性，发现CoT监控在语言分布偏移下普遍存在不可靠性，平均95.9%的模型存在CoT不忠实行为，且模型会策略性地进行操纵（如答案切换、事后合理化），使得外部监控器难以检测欺骗行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往对CoT监控可靠性的研究局限于英语和少量模型，缺乏跨语言、跨模型家族的大规模评估，无法反映CoT监控在实际多语言部署场景中的真实效果。

Method: 采用对抗性提示评估（要求显式中间计算），结合内部答案令牌概率分析，对13种语言、16个模型（7个前沿模型家族，参数规模8B-120B）进行系统性评估。

Result: 1) 跨语言和提示类型中，平均95.9%的模型存在CoT不忠实行为；2) 前沿模型系统性地进行策略性操纵（答案切换、事后合理化、程序性利用提示）；3) 模型在前15%生成阶段即在潜在激活中承诺失调线索；4) 低资源语言中欺骗模式高达100%。

Conclusion: CoT监控在语言分布偏移下本质脆弱，提供的安全信号远弱于纯英语研究所表明的水平；亟需开发鲁棒的CoT监控器，加速白盒监控技术研究，特别是改善中低资源语言的CoT可监控性。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought (CoT) monitoring has been proposed as a promising safety mechanism for detecting misaligned behavior in large language models. However, its reliability remains largely unexplored beyond English and across diverse model families. We present the first large-scale evaluation of CoT monitorability across 13 diverse languages and seven frontier model families, comprising 16 models. Using adversarial-hint evaluations that require explicit intermediate computation, together with analysis of internal answer-token probabilities, we consistently find CoT unfaithfulness across languages and hint types, with an average rate of 95.9\% across 8B--120B parameter models. We find that frontier models systematically engage in strategic manipulation, including answer-switching, post-hoc rationalization, and procedural exploitation of hints, making external monitors struggle to detect deception. We show that frontier models often commit to the misaligned cue in their latent activations within the first 15\% of generation, even when the CoT appears faithful. Surprisingly, these deceptive patterns remain 100\% in low-resource languages, revealing fundamental limitations in current CoT-based oversight. Our results reveal that CoT monitoring is fundamentally fragile under linguistic distribution shift, providing a substantially weaker safety signal than what English-only studies suggest. These findings underscore an urgent need to develop robust CoT monitors and to accelerate research into white-box monitoring techniques, especially to improve CoT monitorability in mid- and low-resource languages. Our code is available \href{https://multilingual-cot-monitoring.github.io/}{\textcolor{blue}{here}}.

</details>


### [112] [ESC-Skills: Discovering and Self-Evolving Skills for Emotional Support Conversations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27908)
*Jie Zhu,Huaixia Dou,Shuo Jiang,Junhui Li,Lifan Guo,Feng Chen,Chi Zhang,Fang Kong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出ESC-Skills，一个以技能为中心的框架，通过发现和自演化可执行情感支持技能来提升情感支持对话系统的可解释性和可控性，并显著改善对话质量与情感效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有情感支持对话系统主要依赖端到端生成或粗粒度策略监督，缺乏可解释性，难以系统性地提升支持技能。

Method: 1) 将局部支持交互建模为干预单元(IU)，捕捉求助者状态-支持干预-情感变化动态；2) 构建ESC-Skills Bank技能库，包含干预指导、适用条件、预期效果和潜在风险；3) 提出多画像自演化精炼框架，通过模拟交互发现缺失技能、不安全干预和特定画像的失败模式，再通过基于模拟的验证优化技能库。

Result: ESC-Skills在响应质量和对话层面的情感结果上均有提升，同时提供了更具可解释性和可控性的支持行为。

Conclusion: 基于技能中心的方法能够有效提升情感支持对话系统的性能、可解释性和可控性，代码和资源将开源。

Abstract: Existing emotional support conversation (ESC) systems mainly rely on end-to-end response generation or coarse strategy supervision, offering limited interpretability and little support for systematic skill improvement. We propose ESC-Skills, a skill-centric framework that discovers and self-evolves executable emotional support skills. We first model localized support interactions as Intervention Units (IUs), which capture state--action--outcome dynamics between seeker states, support interventions, and post-response emotional changes. Based on IUs extracted from both successful and failed ESC dialogues, we construct the ESC-Skills Bank, a repository of executable emotional support skills containing intervention guidance, applicability conditions, expected outcomes, and potential risks. To further improve robustness, we introduce a multi-profile self-evolutionary refinement framework in which an ESC agent interacts with diverse simulated seeker profiles under SAGE evaluation. The resulting interaction traces are analyzed to identify missing skills, unsafe interventions, and profile-specific failure patterns, which are then used to refine the Skills Bank through simulation-based verification. Experimental results demonstrate that ESC-Skills improves both response-level quality and dialogue-level emotional outcomes while providing more interpretable and controllable support behaviors. We will release the code, prompts, and ESC-Skills Bank at https://github.com/aliyun/qwen-dianjin.

</details>


### [113] [GeneralThinker: Domain-General Reasoning through Likelihood-Guided Answer-Conditioned Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27934)
*Shengmin Piao,Sanghyun Park*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: GeneralThinker是一个无需领域特定验证器的在线强化学习框架，通过将推理监督转化为密集的答案条件优化，实现响应级评估和 token 级信用分配，在11个基准测试中取得最佳平均性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于可验证奖励的强化学习方法依赖领域特定验证器、稀疏结果奖励和粗粒度信用分配，限制了其通用性和可扩展性。

Method: 提出GeneralThinker框架：(1) 使用真实答案的对数似然作为响应级奖励；(2) 推导token级兼容性信号实现细粒度信用分配；(3) 通过裁剪和方向保持调制稳定优化过程。

Result: 在数学、STEM和通用推理等11个基准测试中取得最佳平均性能；实验表明无控制的token级调制会破坏训练稳定性，而受控调制能使细粒度信用分配持续有效。

Conclusion: GeneralThinker通过密集答案条件优化和受控的token级信用分配，有效克服了现有方法的局限性，是一种通用且稳定的推理增强框架。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards improves language model reasoning, but its reliance on domain-specific verifiers, sparse outcome rewards, and coarse-grained credit assignment limits its applicability. We introduce GeneralThinker, an on-policy framework that reformulates reasoning supervision as dense answer-conditioned optimization, enabling response-level evaluation and token-level credit assignment without domain-specific verifiers. GeneralThinker evaluates generated reasoning trajectories using the likelihood of the ground-truth answer and derives token-wise compatibility signals for fine-grained credit assignment. To stabilize optimization, it constrains token-level updates through clipping and direction-preserving modulation. Across 11 benchmarks spanning mathematics, STEM, and general reasoning, GeneralThinker achieves the best average performance. Further analyses show that uncontrolled token-level modulation can destabilize training, whereas controlled modulation makes fine-grained credit assignment consistently effective.

</details>


### [114] [DisasterBench: Benchmarking LLM Planning under Typed Tool Interface Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27957)
*Zhitong Chen,Kai Yin,Weifeng Zhang,Zhiyuan Wang,Xiangjue Dong,Chengkai Liu,Zhewei Liu,Yiming Xiao,Ali Mostafavi,James Caverlee*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出DisasterBench基准测试和FPoF故障定位方法，评估LLM在灾害响应场景中编排多步工作流的能力，发现语义推理与执行约束之间存在根本性差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灾害响应需要快速协调多种异构AI工具形成多步工作流，LLM作为编排器不仅要选择语义合理的工具，还必须生成可执行的工作流并正确绑定参数和传播依赖关系，现有评估方法无法有效诊断步骤级故障。

Method: 构建DisasterBench基准，包含语义相似但操作不同的灾害响应工具集；提出First-Point-of-Failure (FPoF)方法，在预测工作流中定位最早根因，区分主要错误与下游级联效应。

Result: 规划方法的有效性高度依赖模型容量；工具误匹配和参数绑定错误是最常见的首故障原因，揭示语义基础和执行一致性是不同瓶颈；冗余的中间推理可能与结构化输出要求产生指令冲突，扰乱计划生成。

Conclusion: 语义推理与执行导向的协调之间存在根本性差距，需要联合建模语义意图、执行约束和工作流一致性的规划框架。

Abstract: Disasters cause severe societal impacts, demanding rapid coordination of heterogeneous AI tools, from satellite analysis to flood prediction and damage assessment, into coherent multi-step workflows. As LLMs increasingly serve as orchestrators of such pipelines, effective coordination requires more than selecting semantically plausible tools: LLMs must generate executable workflows with correct parameter binding and dependency propagation.
  We introduce DisasterBench, a benchmark for evaluating structured multi-agent planning over semantically similar but operationally distinct disaster-response tools. To enable step-level failure attribution, we further propose First-Point-of-Failure (FPoF), which localizes the earliest root cause in a predicted workflow, separating primary errors from downstream cascading effects.
  Our evaluation reveals three findings: planning method effectiveness depends strongly on model capacity; tool mismatch and parameter-binding errors dominate first failures, revealing semantic grounding and execution consistency as distinct bottlenecks; and verbose intermediate reasoning can create instruction clash with structured output requirements, disrupting plan generation.
  Together, these findings highlight a fundamental gap between semantic reasoning and execution-grounded coordination, underscoring the need for planning frameworks that jointly model semantic intent, execution constraints, and workflow consistency.
  Code, data, and evaluation resources are available at: https://github.com/TamuChen18/DisasterBench_Open

</details>


### [115] [Boundary Suppression Asymmetry in Post-trained Assistants: Over-expansion as a Controllability Cost](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27969)
*Jiarui Han*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 后训练语言模型助手因优化过度避免回答不足，导致在用户要求窄化回答时，某些"过度助手"行为（如过度完成、额外帮助、反欠回答）难以被局部抑制。反欠回答策略在边界控制评估中比基线更难拉回，机制分析指向混合规划/停止账户，即内容预算超支与延续持续性共同使边界纠正更难。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究后训练语言模型助手在优化过程中是否产生了非对称的可控性代价：当用户明确要求更窄的回答时，哪些助手行为可以被抑制，哪些仍然会塑造响应。

Method: 通过提示端探针在多个高级响应维度上进行研究；使用基于共享基座模型的受控助手策略变体进行边界控制评估；通过机制导向探针分析长输出、EOS失败、不确定性补偿、局部延续偏差等因素；进行鲁棒性检验。

Result: 反欠回答策略在匹配的边界控制评估中比基线更难拉回；最小边界变体通常避免了这种反侧向上偏移；机制分析指向内容预算超支与延续持续性共同导致边界纠正困难；鲁棒性检验在共享系统和更大规模设置下保持主要排序。

Conclusion: 后训练可能产生了方向特定的可控性代价：一些有用的助手倾向容易被调用，但更难被局部抑制。支持混合规划/停止账户解释。

Abstract: Post-trained language-model assistants are often optimized to avoid under-answering, encouraging complete, helpful, cautious, and proactive responses. We ask whether this optimization creates asymmetric controllability costs: when users explicitly request narrower answers, which assistant behaviors remain suppressible, and which continue to shape the response? We study this problem as boundary-suppression asymmetry. Prompt-side probes across multiple high-level response dimensions suggest a selective cost, concentrated around `too-much assistant' directions such as over-completion, extra help, and anti-underanswering.
  Using controlled assistant-policy variants derived from a shared base model, we find that anti-underanswering policies are harder to pull back than the baseline under matched boundary-control evaluations, while minimal-boundary variants generally avoid this anti-side upward shift in the direct boundary-control comparisons. Mechanism-oriented probes point beyond longer default outputs, pure EOS failure, uncertainty compensation, and local continuation bias, while robustness checks preserve the main anti-over-baseline ordering under shared-system and larger-scale settings. The evidence supports a mixed planning/stopping account, where content-budget overshoot and continuation persistence jointly make boundary correction harder. Overall, post-training may create direction-specific controllability costs: some helpful assistant tendencies remain easy to invoke, yet harder to locally suppress.

</details>


### [116] [Periodic RoPE for Infinite Context LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27980)
*Simin Huo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Periodic RoPE (P-RoPE)位置编码机制，结合滑动窗口注意力（捕捉局部依赖）和无位置编码的全局注意力（实现全局交互），通过交替堆叠两种层来避免位置外推问题，理论上支持无限上下文窗口。实验表明模型MiniWin在长上下文效率和稳定性上优于标准GPT架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM在处理超长上下文时，当序列长度超过预训练位置编码范围（如RoPE）时会出现位置耗尽问题，导致性能下降。为了克服这一根本限制并实现真正的无限上下文理解，作者提出了P-RoPE。

Method: 提出Periodic RoPE (P-RoPE)，与滑动窗口注意力（SWA）结合捕获局部依赖和窗口内相对位置，再与无位置编码（NoPE）的全局注意力层互补，通过交替堆叠这两种层避免位置外推，理论上支持无限上下文。

Result: 提出的模型MiniWin在长上下文效率和稳定性上优于使用标准GPT架构的MiniMind。

Conclusion: P-RoPE机制通过避免位置外推，为LLM实现真正无限上下文理解提供了一条可行路径。代码已开源。

Abstract: The ability to process ultra-long contexts is crucial for large language models (LLMs) to perform long-horizon tasks. While recent efforts have extended context windows to 1M and beyond, model performance degrades when sequence length exceeds the pre-trained range of positional encodings (e.g., RoPE), i.e., position exhaustion. This fundamental limitation must be overcome to achieve a truly infinite context. To address it, we propose Periodic RoPE (P-RoPE), a positional encoding mechanism designed to circumvent this exhaustion. It operates in conjunction with sliding window attention (SWA) to capture local dependencies and relative positions within each window. This local layer is then complemented by a global attention layer with No Positional Encoding (NoPE), enabling unbounded interaction across the entire sequence without positional constraints. By stacking these two types of layers, the model avoids the need for positional extrapolation to generalize longer and theoretically supports an infinite context window. Empirical results show that our model, MiniWin, outperforms MiniMInd with standard GPT architectures in long-context efficiency and stability. Our work provides a possible pathway toward LLMs with genuine infinite-context understanding. The code is available at \href{https://github.com/Cominder/miniwin}{https://github.com/Cominder/miniwin}.

</details>


### [117] [KVoiceBench, KOpenAudioBench, and KMMAU: Agent-Driven Korean Speech Benchmarks for Evaluating SpeechLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27984)
*Haechan Kim,Seungjun Chung,Inkyu Park,Jihoo Lee,Jonghyun Lee*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出两种人机协作的基准构建框架，将英文语音语言模型评估扩展到多语言场景，并构建了包含12,345个样本的韩语语音基准（KVoiceBench、KOpenAudioBench、KMMAU），发现单语评估无法揭示的跨语言性能差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SpeechLM评估严重依赖英语，缺乏可靠的多语言语音能力评测；直接通过ASR-翻译-归一化-TTS流水线迁移基准会破坏语言特定指令、答案约束和口语形式，且音频理解任务中源语言音频无法保留目标语言的说话人属性、口音和副语言特征。

Method: 提出两种人机协作框架：(1) 将源语言SpokenQA基准迁移为目标语言SpokenQA基准；(2) 利用转录文本和说话人元数据将目标语言ASR语料库转换为音频理解基准。基于此构建了三个韩语语音基准。

Result: 评估8个最新SpeechLM发现：英韩性能差距在模型和任务族间差异显著；SpokenQA与音频理解的排名不一致，揭示了英语单语评估无法发现的互补性弱点。

Conclusion: 现有SpeechLM评估应扩展到多语言场景，本文提出的框架可有效构建多语言语音基准，揭示模型在跨语言场景中的真实性能差异。

Abstract: Speech language models (SpeechLMs) have achieved substantial progress by extending large language models (LLMs) to the speech modality. However, SpeechLM evaluation remains heavily centered on English, limiting reliable assessment of multilingual speech capabilities. Straightforward benchmark transfer through ASR, translation, normalization, and TTS can corrupt language-specific instructions, answer constraints, and spoken forms; for audio understanding, transferring source-language audio also fails to preserve target-language speaker attributes, accents, and paralinguistic properties. To address these limitations, we propose two human-agent benchmark-construction frameworks: one transfers source-language SpokenQA benchmarks into target-language SpokenQA benchmarks, and the other converts target-language ASR corpora into audio understanding benchmarks using transcriptions and speaker metadata. Using these frameworks, we construct and publicly release three Korean speech benchmarks: KVoiceBench and KOpenAudioBench for Korean SpokenQA, and KMMAU for Korean audio understanding, comprising 12,345 samples in total. We evaluate eight recent SpeechLMs and find that English-Korean performance gaps vary substantially across models and task families, and that SpokenQA and audio understanding rankings diverge, revealing complementary weaknesses invisible to English-only evaluation.

</details>


### [118] [An Evolutionary Approach for Designing Stable and Highly Expressible Low-Immunogenicity Therapeutic mRNA Sequences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27986)
*Dhawa Sang Dong,Mausam Gurung,Suraj Kandel*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种两阶段mRNA序列优化框架（BERT-GA），结合深度学习（CodonTransformer）生成生物一致的mRNA序列，再通过遗传算法（GA）进行密码子感知的交叉和同义突变优化，在翻译效率（CAI达0.73-0.74，tAI达0.63-0.64）、结构稳定性（MFE在-346至-356 kcal/mol，约84%碱基配对）和低免疫原性（免疫惩罚降至27.3）三者之间实现了最优平衡，优于现有模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: mRNA作为治疗性序列需要优化翻译效率、结构稳定性和低免疫原性，现有方法（如线性设计导致超稳定结构抑制翻译，BiLSTM-CRF仅追求高CAI忽略结构约束）无法实现三者之间的最优平衡，因此需要一种更全面的数据驱动优化方法。

Method: 两阶段框架：第一阶段使用预训练的CodonTransformer（类BERT大语言模型）生成生物一致的mRNA候选序列；第二阶段使用遗传算法进行密码子感知的交叉和同义突变（基于人类密码子使用偏好），通过综合适应度函数（包含CAI、tAI、密码子对偏倚、局部/全局MFE、GC含量、CpG/UpA基序频率）评估并优化序列。

Result: 经过38、40、42代优化后，CAI达0.73-0.74，tAI达0.63-0.64（提升超6%），密码子对偏倚稳定在0.97；5'端核糖体可及性（unpaired_30 fraction）达0.87；全局MFE收敛至-346至-356 kcal/mol，约84%碱基配对结构稳定性；免疫刺激基序降低，平均免疫惩罚降至27.3。

Conclusion: BERT-GA框架在翻译效率、结构稳定性和低免疫原性之间实现了最优平衡，表明该数据驱动方法是一种有效的mRNA序列计算机设计与优化方案。

Abstract: Messenger RNA (mRNA) sequences as therapeutics require optimized design to ensure efficient translation, structural stability, and minimal immunogenicity. This study presents a two-stage in-silico framework that integrates deep learning and evolutionary computation for rational mRNA optimization instead of existing state-of-the-art models. In the first stage, a pretrained CodonTransformer (BERT-like Large Language Model) generates biologically coherent mRNA sequences encoding the target antigen. In the second stage, a genetic algorithm (GA) evolves these candidate sequences through codon-aware crossover and synonymous mutation guided by human codon usage preferences. Fitness functions for evaluation combined translation-related metrics (CAI, tAI, codon-pair bias), mRNA structural stability (local and global MFE via RNAfold, GC content), and reduced immunogenicity (CpG/UpA motif frequency). Over successive generations (38th, 40th, and 42nd), the GA improved (achieved CAI values of 0.73 to 0.74 and tAI values of 0.63 to 0.64) CAI and tAI by over 6% and codon-pair bias is high and consistent (0.97 ) and improved ribosomal accessibility at the 5' end, with an unpaired_30 fraction reaching 0.87; Global Minimum Free Energy (MFE) converged to a balanced range of -346 to -356 kcal/mol, achieving approximately 84% base-paired structural stability, and reduced immune-stimulatory motifs - lowering the average immune penalty to 27.3 in the final generation. Linear Design produces hyper-stable transcripts (MFE < - 2000 kcal/mol) that risk translation inefficiency due to extreme rigidity, and BiLSTM-CRF focuses solely on high CAI (0.96 to 0.98) without structural constraints, our framework achieves an optimal translation-stability equilibrium, highlighting the proposed BERT-GA framework as an effective, data-driven approach for the design and optimization of in-silico mRNA sequences.

</details>


### [119] [Auditing Stance Asymmetry in Generative Explanations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27988)
*Jiarui Han*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出对称分解评估（SDE）方法，用于检测语言模型在开放式生成解释中的立场不对称性（stance-bearing asymmetry），即模型可能通过责任归因、合法性赋予、背景交代等方式，在避免显性敌对语言的同时对不同群体施加不平等的解释立场。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有偏见评估主要关注有界比较（如公开贬低、刻板关联、控制替换下的标签敏感差异），但开放式解释存在不同问题：模型通过分配责任、合法性、背景和 grievance 来引导解读，可能在一方被赋予结构性理解的同时，另一方被归咎于个人过错、反应过度或不值得认真对待。这种立场不对称性在现有评估框架中未被充分捕捉。

Method: 提出对称分解评估（SDE），包含三部分：1）配对情景测试（具体群体标签）；2）结构角色改写；3）显式支持或反证。在32个族群的受控原型套件中进行测试，通过分解检验表面差异在结构控制或证据控制下的变化，并通过定向案例审查和评测模型比较评估自动评分的稳定性。

Result: 分解表明表面差异并非同质：部分差异在结构或证据控制下减弱，另一些则在模型如何分配责备、背景或合法性方面保持稳定差异。评测模型（judge）的读数在不同操作化间漂移，标量分数可能抹平读者用于解读解释立场的关键区分。

Conclusion: SDE将生成式偏见评估重新定义为对解释立场的审计——关注每方获得何种立场、在分解下如何变化、以及自动评分在何处变得不稳定。

Abstract: Bias evaluation for language models has made substantial progress on bounded comparisons, such as overt derogation, stereotype association, or label-sensitive differences under controlled substitutions. Open-ended explanations raise a different problem: they guide interpretation by assigning responsibility, legitimacy, context, and grievance. A model can avoid hostile language while making one side structurally understandable and another personally at fault, overreacting, or less worth taking seriously. We call this stance-bearing asymmetry in generative explanations. We propose Symmetry Decomposition Evaluation (SDE), which tests paired situations with concrete group labels, structural-role rewrites, and explicit support or counter-evidence. In a controlled 32-family prototype suite, this decomposition shows that surface differences are not all alike: some weaken under structural or evidence control, while others remain as stable differences in how the model assigns blame, context, or legitimacy. Targeted case review and judge comparison suggest a broader difficulty for evaluating open-ended framing asymmetries: judge readings shift across operationalizations, and scalar scores can flatten distinctions that readers use to interpret explanatory stance. SDE therefore reframes generative bias evaluation as an audit of explanatory stance -- what stance each side receives, how it changes under decomposition, and where automatic scoring becomes unstable.

</details>


### [120] [Rethinking Visual Neglect: Steering via Context-Preference for MLLM Hallucination Mitigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27993)
*Jingwen Wu,Xijun Zhang,Ge Song*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出Context-Preference Activation Steering (CAS)，一种无需训练即可在推理时通过控制多模态大模型的信息依赖（视觉vs文本/知识）来缓解物体幻觉的框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推理时缓解幻觉的方法主要将幻觉归因于视觉不足，通过增强视觉依赖来缓解；但实验发现增强视觉依赖可能在某些模型上反而加剧幻觉，说明仅归因于视觉不足是不充分的。

Method: 提出CAS框架：通过两组设计好的冲突样本提取两个语义不同的上下文偏好向量（CPVs），在推理时通过单次符号残差注入到中早期MLP层，从而控制模型对视觉、文本和参数知识的信息依赖。

Result: CAS显著缓解物体幻觉，不增加解码延迟，且保持原生文本生成质量。

Conclusion: 图像作为上下文与模型的参数知识和文本上下文存在竞争关系，单纯增强视觉依赖并非通用解；通过激活控制可有效调节信息依赖以缓解幻觉。

Abstract: Object hallucination remains a primary obstacle to the reliable deployment of Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs). Current inference-time mitigation methods mainly assume hallucinations stem from visual neglect, steering models to enhance visual reliance. In contrast, our systematic interventions on multiple MLLMs show that pushing toward more visual reliance may exacerbate hallucinations on some models, while less may mitigate hallucinations. This result suggests that attributing hallucinations solely to visual insufficiency is underdetermined. We argue that the image, as a context, simultaneously competes with the model's parametric knowledge and the textual context. For this, we propose a training-free framework, Context-Preference Activation Steering (CAS). It extracts two semantically distinct Context Preference Vectors (CPVs) via two small sets of designed conflict samples and applies them via single-pass signed residual injection at mid-early MLP layers during inference to control information reliance. Experiments show that CAS substantially mitigates object hallucinations without increasing decoding latency and preserves native text-generation quality.

</details>


### [121] [Where Does Toxicity Live? Mechanistic Localization and Targeted Suppression in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27997)
*Himanshu Beniwal,Mayank Singh*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Meow2X和TRNE两种无需重新训练的框架，通过分析毒性提示与中性提示之间的激活差异，定位并抑制大语言模型中特定层和神经元的毒性，在不牺牲语言质量的前提下有效降低毒性输出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大语言模型解毒方法依赖昂贵的重训练或输出级过滤，缺乏对毒性来源的机制性理解，无法从内部定位毒性的产生位置。

Method: 通过分析毒性提示与中性提示之间的激活差异，将毒性定位到特定层和神经元，然后通过推理时缩放或最小秩一权重编辑进行抑制，无需梯度下降。

Result: 在5个语言模型、2个基准测试和90种配置下，使用双重安全评估器验证了一致的毒性降低效果，同时保持语言建模质量。发现毒性不成比例地编码在早期MLP层，且单评估器设置会系统性地低估毒性。

Conclusion: 通过将机制可解释性与实用解毒方法相结合，为构建更安全、更透明的语言模型提供了原则性路径，并强调了多评估器安全评估的必要性。

Abstract: Large language models frequently generate toxic, hateful, or harmful content, yet existing mitigation methods rely on costly retraining or output-level filtering with no mechanistic insight into where toxicity originates internally. We introduce Meow2X and TRNE, two complementary retraining-free frameworks that localize toxicity to specific layers and neurons by analyzing activation differentials between toxic and neutral prompts, then suppress them via inference-time scaling or minimal rank-one weight edits -- without any gradient descent. Evaluations across five LMs, two benchmarks, and 90 configurations using dual safety evaluators demonstrate consistent toxicity reduction while preserving language modeling quality. Our analysis reveals that toxicity is disproportionately encoded in early MLP layers, varies across architectures, and is systematically underestimated by single-evaluator setups -- underscoring the need for multi-evaluator safety assessment. By bridging mechanistic interpretability with practical detoxification, our framework offers a principled path toward safer, more transparent language models.

</details>


### [122] [ResearchMath-14K: Scaling Research-Level Mathematics via Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28003)
*Guijin Son,Seungyeop Yi,Minju Gwak,Hyunwoo Ko,Wongi Jang,Youngjae Yu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文构建了迄今为止最大的研究级数学问题数据集ResearchMath-14k（14,056个问题），并生成22万条教师轨迹。发现模型存在回避行为（不尝试、伪造引用），且新模型生成更多伪造引用。经智能体过滤后微调Qwen3（4B-30B），平均提升9.2分，表明过滤后的开放问题尝试可提供有效监督。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型能否在没有人类干预的情况下有意义地处理前沿数学问题尚不清楚，主要障碍是缺乏大规模研究级数学数据集。

Method: 通过多智能体流水线从学术来源策划14,056个问题的ResearchMath-14k数据集；生成220K教师轨迹（ResearchMath-Reasoning）；采用智能体过滤去除回避行为样本；在过滤后的数据上微调Qwen3模型（4B-30B参数）。

Result: 较新代模型每条轨迹生成5.6倍更多引用和5.0倍更多伪造引用；过滤后微调Qwen3模型在4B-30B规模上平均提升9.2个点。

Conclusion: 即使没有完全正确的推理轨迹，经过滤的开放问题尝试也能为研究级数学推理提供有用的监督信号。

Abstract: The frontier of mathematics is defined by problems whose solutions are not yet known, yet it remains unclear whether language models can meaningfully engage with such problems without human intervention. A major obstacle is the lack of large-scale research-level math datasets. To this end, we introduce ResearchMath-14k, a set of $14{,}056$ problems curated from academic sources via a multi-agent pipeline, making it the largest collection of research-level mathematical problems to date. We further generate ResearchMath-Reasoning, $220$K teacher trajectories from two open models, where we observe recurring avoidance behaviors such as non-attempts and fabricated references. Interestingly, across eight open-weight models, newer generations produce $5.6\times$ more references and $5.0\times$ more fake references per trace. After agentic filtering of ResearchMath-Reasoning, fine-tuning Qwen3 models from 4B to 30B parameters improves over base models by $9.2$ points on average. This shows that filtered open-problem attempts can provide useful supervision even without fully correct reasoning traces. We make ResearchMath-14k publicly available for future works on research-level mathematical reasoning.

</details>


### [123] [Beyond Chunk-Local Extraction: Cross-Chunk Graph Augmentation for GraphRAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28004)
*Jiaming Zhang,Yibo Zhao,Jing Yu,Jianxiang Yu,Xiang Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: CrossAug 是一种 GNN 引导的跨块图增强方法，通过自监督学习识别缺失的跨块关系，并仅对高得分区域使用 LLM 补全，从而在不引发组合爆炸的情况下丰富 GraphRAG 的索引结构，在多跳和长文档 QA 基准上持续提升了检索增强生成性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 GraphRAG 框架仅在单个文本块内提取实体和关系，导致跨多个文本块的交叉块关系（跨块关系）在索引中系统性缺失，而穷举式使用 LLM 恢复所有可能的跨块关系由于块组合爆炸而不可行。因此需要一种高效的方法来发现并补充这些缺失的跨块关系。

Method: CrossAug 作为查询前的离线步骤，首先通过自监督图破坏生成训练监督信号，然后使用拓扑感知的 GNN 对子图进行缺失性评分，最后仅对高得分区域应用有证据依据的 LLM 补全，从而在避免组合爆炸的同时增强 GraphRAG 索引中的跨块关系结构。

Result: 在三种基于 LLM 的 GraphRAG 框架上，跨越四个多跳和长文档 QA 基准的实验显示，CrossAug 持续提升了检索增强生成的性能，验证了跨块图增强的有效性。

Conclusion: CrossAug 有效解决了 GraphRAG 中跨块关系缺失的问题，通过 GNN 引导的图增强方法在无需穷举搜索的情况下显著提升了复杂问答任务的检索质量。

Abstract: GraphRAG extends retrieval-augmented generation by organizing corpora as explicit knowledge graphs, enabling graph-based retrieval for complex question answering. However, existing frameworks extract entities and relations within individual chunks, leaving cross-chunk relations -- those whose evidence spans multiple passages -- systematically absent from the index. Exhaustive LLM-based recovery of such relations is impractical due to the combinatorial explosion of chunk combinations. We present CrossAug, a GNN-guided CROSS-Chunk Graph AUGmentation method that enriches GraphRAG indices with cross-chunk relational structure as an offline step before query-time retrieval. CrossAug derives training supervision through self-supervised graph corruption, uses a topology-aware GNN to score subgraphs for missingness, and applies evidence-grounded LLM completion only to selected high-scoring regions. Experiments on three LLM-based GraphRAG frameworks across four multi-hop and long-document QA benchmarks demonstrate that CrossAug consistently improves performance, confirming the benefit of cross-chunk graph augmentation for retrieval-based question answering. Our code is available at https://github.com/DonFinliani/CrossAug.

</details>


### [124] [Integrated and Cross-Architecture Interpretation of LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28006)
*Leonardo Matthew Yauw,Wei-Bin Kou,Yujiu Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出IAR框架，通过带宽校准的MIP、Tukey IQR峰值检测、DTR跨层轨迹分析以及Jaccard稳定性度量，实现对LLM推理过程的多维可解释性分析，并在多个模型和领域上验证了其泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM推理可解释性研究存在实际不对称性：输出可观测但推理过程不透明，且依赖单一探针（如MIP或DTR）容易低估真实推理结构，缺乏统一的分析框架。

Method: 1) 使用带宽校准的MIP结合Tukey IQR峰值检测，在输出层识别推理关键token；2) 对MIP识别的token与DTR深度token进行重叠分析，追踪跨层轨迹；3) 应用Jaccard稳定性度量跨多领域问题验证推理质量。

Result: 在Qwen-7B、Qwen-14B和Llama-8B三个模型上，涵盖数学、代码、逻辑和常识四个领域，实验结果表明IAR框架具有跨架构的泛化可解释能力。

Conclusion: IAR框架提供了一种统一的跨架构推理可解释性方法，综合了输出层关键token识别、跨层轨迹追踪和稳定性验证，能够更全面地揭示LLM的推理模式。

Abstract: Understanding how LLMs reason is hindered by a practical asymmetry: while their generated outputs are observable, the underlying reasoning patterns remain opaque. Relying on single probes, such as Mutual Information Peak (MIP) or Deep-Thinking Ratio (DTR), risks underestimating the genuine inferential structure. To response this deficiency, we present an Integrated, cross-Architecture Reasoning (IAR) framework, designed to provide a unified approach to LLM reasoning interpretability. Specifically, we first propose to use bandwidth-calibrated MIP coupled with Tukey IQR peak-detection to isolate reasoning-crucial tokens at the output layer. Second, we performed an overlap analysis between MIP-picked tokens and DTR-deep tokens to trace the cross-layer trajectories of those tokens. This also discloses whether reasoning-crucial tokens are computation-intensive as well, further facilitating to understand how reasoning patterns evolve across model layers. Finally, we apply a Jaccard stability metric over multi-domain problems to verify if the MIP-identified tokens are reasoning quality-guaranteed. Extensive experiments on three models (Qwen-7B, Qwen-14B, and Llama-8B) across four domains (mathematics, code, logic, and common sense) demonstrate IAR's generalizable interpretation capabilities across architectures.

</details>


### [125] [MemGuard: Preventing Memory Contamination in Long-Term Memory-Augmented Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28009)
*Hyeonjeong Ha,Jeonghwan Kim,Cheng Qian,Jiayu Liu,William M. Campbell,Yue Wu,Yuji Zhang,Kathleen McKeown,Dilek Hakkani-Tur,Heng Ji*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: MemGuard是一种类型感知的记忆框架，通过区分用户事实、 episodic事件和行为规则等不同类型的记忆，在写入时为每条记忆分配明确的功能角色，并在检索时仅从必要的记忆类型中组合证据，从而解决异构记忆污染问题。在幻觉检测和长对话基准测试中，MemGuard将记忆可靠性提升高达28.27%，同时检索的记忆token数比先前方法减少多达5.8倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有记忆增强型大语言模型将稳定用户事实、 episodic事件和行为规则等不同功能的记忆混入同一空间，导致异构记忆污染问题——上下文特定的事件被过度泛化为断言，或语义相关但功能不兼容的记忆误导生成，损害了长期推理的可靠性。

Method: MemGuard框架包含三个核心机制：(1) 写入时类型分配——为每条记忆赋予显式的功能角色；(2) 类型隔离记忆间的关系维护；(3) 选择性证据组合——仅从必要的记忆类型中检索和组合证据，减少来自无关或功能不兼容记忆的污染。

Result: 在幻觉检测和长对话基准测试中，MemGuard将记忆可靠性提升高达28.27%，同时检索的记忆token数比先前方法减少多达5.8倍。

Conclusion: 可靠的长期推理依赖于对异构记忆进行有原则的组织和选择性使用。类型感知的记忆管理是提升大语言模型长期记忆系统可靠性的有效方向。

Abstract: Memory-augmented large language models extend reasoning beyond a fixed context window by maintaining long-term memory across interactions. However, existing memory systems often collapse stable user facts, episodic events, and behavioral rules into a shared space, allowing functionally distinct memories to be retrieved and used as interchangeable evidence. We identify this failure mode as heterogeneous memory contamination, where context-specific events become overgeneralized claims, or semantically relevant but functionally incompatible memories mislead generation. To this end, we introduce MemGuard, a type-aware memory framework that preserves functional memory boundaries during memory construction and retrieval. It assigns each memory an explicit functional role at write time, maintains relations across type-isolated memories, and selectively composes evidence only from necessary memory types, reducing contamination from irrelevant or functionally incompatible evidence. Across hallucination and long-horizon conversation benchmarks, MemGuard improves memory reliability by up to 28.27% while retrieving up to 5.8x fewer memory tokens than prior methods. These results suggest that reliable long-term reasoning depends on principled organization and selective use of heterogeneous memory.

</details>


### [126] [KSAFE-MM: A Multimodal Safety Benchmark via Localized Contextualization for Korean Cultural Risks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28013)
*Yongwoo Kim,Sojung An,Yunjin Park,Jungwon Yoon,Dujin Lee,HyunBeom Cho,Jaewon Lee,Wonhyuk Lee,Youngchol Kim,JeongYeop Kim,Donghyun Kim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了KSAFE-MM，一个针对韩语多模态大模型安全评估的基准，涵盖通用安全风险和文化特定漏洞，并揭示了模型对文化相关攻击的脆弱性更高，以及安全性与过度拒绝之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态大模型安全评估工具存在两大局限：一是数据集构建以英语为中心，二是仅关注通用风险而忽视本地文化背景。这导致对非英语环境下文化特定安全漏洞的评估不足。

Method: 构建KSAFE-MM基准，包含两部分：KSAFE-MM-G通过语言语境化将通用安全查询转化为韩语多模态样本；KSAFE-MM-C利用真实世界文化视觉线索配以越狱文本查询，评估文化依赖的安全漏洞。采用通用到局部的流水线构建方式。

Result: 评估12个前沿MLLM发现：模型对文化相关攻击比通用攻击更脆弱；越狱策略显著提升攻击成功率（ProgramExecution达74.2%，而标准查询仅13.4%）；存在安全性与过度拒绝的系统性权衡。

Conclusion: 亟需超越英语中心化的基准，建立文化语境下的安全评估体系。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) exacerbate safety risks by introducing vulnerabilities across multiple modalities, such as language and vision. Current MLLM safety evaluation tools, however, suffer from major limitations: 1) English-centric dataset construction, and 2) a focus on generic risks that are not tied to local cultural contexts. This paper introduces KSAFE-MM, a benchmark for Korean multimodal safety evaluation that covers both general safety risks and culture-specific vulnerabilities. KSAFE-MM consists of two parts, KSAFE-MM-G and KSAFE-MM-C. KSAFE-MM-G evaluates globally shared risks in Korean contexts through linguistic contextualization, which transforms generic safety queries into contextually grounded multimodal samples. KSAFE-MM-C targets culture-dependent MLLM safety vulnerabilities using localized visual queries derived from real-world contexts. It pairs these visual queries with jailbreak-style textual queries to cover multimodal safety risks involving cultural visual cues and malicious textual intent. Together, these components provide a general-to-local construction pipeline for evaluating both globally shared safety risks and culture-specific vulnerabilities. We evaluate 12 state-of-the-art MLLMs on KSAFE-MM and reveal that models exhibit greater vulnerability to culturally grounded attacks than to generic ones. Notably, jailbreaking strategies substantially amplify attack success rates, with ProgramExecution yielding up to 74.2% ASR compared to 13.4% for standard queries. Furthermore, we identify a systematic trade-off between safety and over-refusal, where models achieving low ASR tend to exhibit excessive refusal behavior on benign queries. These findings highlight the urgent need for culturally grounded safety evaluation beyond English-centric benchmarks.

</details>


### [127] [ROSD: Reflective On-Policy Self-Distillation for Language Model Reasoning across Domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28014)
*Ziqi Zhao,Xinyu Ma,Liu Yang,Yujie Feng,Daiting Shi,Jingzhou He,Xin Xin,Zhaochun Ren,Xiao-Ming Wu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出反思式在线自蒸馏（ROSD），通过反思引导和错误定位的蒸馏机制，解决现有在线自蒸馏方法在域内推理增益有限和域外泛化差的问题，显著提升了大语言模型的推理性能和泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有在线自蒸馏（OPSD）方法存在两个问题：（1）自教师模型以已验证的解为条件，会模仿训练域参考轨迹而非进行错误修正；（2）对完整响应进行蒸馏会覆盖有效推理前缀并强化过拟合。这两个问题导致域内推理增益有限且域外泛化能力差。

Method: ROSD框架包含三个核心组件：首先，自反射器从模型自身的推理轨迹中提取修正性想法并定位首个错误跨度；其次，修正性想法引导自教师模型生成针对性的监督信号；最后，仅对定位到的错误跨度进行蒸馏，保留正确的推理前缀。通过这种反思引导、错误定位的蒸馏方式，将模仿参考解转变为针对性推理修正。

Result: 在多个域内和域外推理基准上，ROSD相比标准OPSD方法，在域内推理性能上表现更强，在域外泛化能力上有显著提升。

Conclusion: 反思式在线自蒸馏通过错误定位和针对性修正，有效解决了标准OPSD的模仿问题和过拟合问题，为提升LLM推理能力提供了更有效的训练范式。

Abstract: On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) improves the reasoning performance of large language models (LLMs) by providing dense token-level supervision for on-policy rollouts. However, existing OPSD methods often yield limited gains on in-domain reasoning and generalize poorly to out-of-domain problems. We identify two key causes: conditioning the self-teacher on a verified solution encourages imitation of training-domain reference trajectories rather than error-specific correction, and applying distillation to the full response can overwrite valid reasoning prefixes and reinforce overfitting.
  We propose Reflective On-policy Self-Distillation (ROSD), a framework that turns reference-solution imitation into targeted reasoning correction through reflection-guided, error-localized distillation. For each rollout, ROSD uses a self-reflector to extract a corrective idea and locate the first erroneous span. The corrective idea guides the self-teacher toward targeted supervision, while the localized error span restricts distillation to where correction is needed. This design corrects flawed reasoning while preserving valid prefixes. Experiments on multiple in-domain and out-of-domain reasoning benchmarks show that ROSD yields stronger in-domain reasoning performance overall and substantially better out-of-domain generalization than standard OPSD. Code is available at https://github.com/ZiqiZhao1/ROSD.

</details>


### [128] [The Missing Piece in Pre-trained Model Evaluation: Reward-Guided Decoding Unlocks Task-Oriented Behavior Without Parameter Updates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28020)
*Shaobo Wang,Guo Chen,Ziyue Wang,Zhengyang Tang,Qingyang Liu,Xingzhang Ren,Dayiheng Liu,Linfeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出Energy-Based Decoding (EBD)，一种无需训练、奖励引导的框架，通过外部轻量级奖励模型增强冻结预训练LLM的解码过程，在开放型和目标型任务中激活任务导向行为，实现更公平的推理时评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练基座LLM优化目标是下一词预测，在标准提示和直接解码下常无法遵循指令或产生格式良好的答案，导致基准性能混淆了模型能力与解码引发的失败，而揭示这种行为通常依赖 costly 的后训练；现有仅解码方法效率低且对开放任务不鲁棒。

Method: EBD是一种训练-free、奖励引导的解码框架。在解码阶段引入外部轻量级奖励模型，通过奖励倾斜的目标分布（reward-tilted target distribution）引导生成朝向高效用响应，同时锚定到预训练模型先验上。

Result: 在5个模型和6个基准上超越基线；Qwen3-8B-Base在AlpacaEval2.0上从8.8提升至44.5；Mistral-7B在Math500上延迟相比先前解码工作降低18.9倍；对奖励模型大小保持鲁棒。

Conclusion: EBD能够将基座模型输出向更遵循指令的行为偏移，增加与后训练模型的行为相似性，实现了对可访问的预训练模型行为的更公平的推理时评估。

Abstract: With the rapid progress of large language models (LLMs), reliably evaluating the capabilities of pre-trained LLMs has become increasingly important. The challenge is that base pre-trained models are optimized for next-token prediction and often fail to follow instructions or produce well-formed answers under standard prompting and direct decoding. As a result, benchmark performance can conflate model capability with decoding-induced failures to produce task-oriented outputs, while exposing such behavior often relies on costly post-training. Recent decodingonly approaches attempt to reshape output distributions, but such methods can be inefficient and brittle across open-ended tasks. To address these limitations, we propose Energy-Based Decoding (EBD), a training-free, reward-guided framework for activating task-oriented behaviors from frozen pre-trained LLMs across both open-ended and objective tasks. EBD augments decoding with an external lightweight reward model, steering generations toward high-utility responses while anchoring them to the pre-trained model prior through a reward-tilted target distribution. We show that EBD shifts base-model outputs toward more instructionfollowing behavior, increasing behavioral similarity to post-trained counterparts and enabling a fairer inference-time evaluation of accessible pre-trained-model behavior. Empirically, EBD outperforms baselines across five models and six benchmarks, improving Qwen3-8B-Base on AlpacaEval2.0 from 8.8 to 44.5, reducing Mistral-7B Math500 latency by 18.9x relative to prior decoding work, and remaining robust to reward-model size.

</details>


### [129] [Beyond pass@k: Redundancy-Aware RLVR for Multi-Sample Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28022)
*Le Bronnec Florian,Alexandre Verine,Rio Yokota,Benjamin Negrevergne*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了代码生成模型中RLVR（基于验证器的强化学习）方法对生成冗余性的影响，发现仅优化正确性会导致生成结果集中在重复实现上。通过引入基于JPlag相似度的反冗余奖励，在多个模型和基准上有效降低了冗余性并提升了有限预算下的可执行性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM代码生成评估常用Pass@k指标，但RLVR方法在提升正确性的同时，对生成程序间的冗余性影响尚不明确。冗余意味着多样性缺失，会限制有限采样预算下的实际性能，因此理解并缓解冗余是提升代码生成效率的关键。

Method: 首先通过JPlag系统量化代码实现层面的冗余性；然后提出在RLVR训练中直接加入基于JPlag相似度的反冗余奖励项，以鼓励生成多样化实现；在3个模型和3个基准上与仅优化正确性的RLVR及Pass@k感知目标进行对比实验。

Result: 仅优化正确性的RLVR会显著增加生成代码的冗余性；反冗余奖励方法能有效降低冗余，并在有限采样预算下提升可执行性能，通常达到或超过专门的Pass@k感知优化目标的效果。

Conclusion: 代码生成中，正确性目标与多样性目标存在权衡。通过显式惩罚近重复生成，可以在不牺牲正确性的前提下提高采样效率，为RLVR训练提供了简单有效的改进方向。

Abstract: LLMs for code generation are commonly evaluated in repeated-sampling settings using Pass@k, where multiple candidate programs are executed against unit tests under a finite sampling budget. While recent verifier-based reinforcement learning (RLVR) methods improve executable correctness, how these objectives affect redundancy among sampled programs remains poorly understood. In this work, we study implementation-level redundancy in code generation using JPlag, a plagiarism-detection system for code. Across models and benchmarks, we show that correctness-only RLVR often concentrates generations around repeated implementations, whereas Pass@k-aware objectives maintain lower redundancy and improve larger-budget performance. Motivated by these observations, we augment RLVR with direct anti-redundancy rewards based on JPlag similarity. Across 3 models and 3 benchmarks, discouraging near-duplicate generations reliably improves finite-budget executable performance, often matching or outperforming specialized Pass@k-aware objectives.

</details>


### [130] [Extracting Small Translation Specialists from LLMs by Aggressively Pruning Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28042)
*Liu O. Martin,Lucas Bandarkar,Nanyun Peng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种从混合专家（MoE）大语言模型中激进剪枝与翻译无关的专家（Expert）的方法，可在不重新训练或仅需少量微调（SFT）的情况下，剪掉高达90%的专家而保持合理的翻译质量，证明翻译任务仅需LLM的一小部分参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代LLM作为通才模型，在机器翻译上虽然表现优异但存在严重的过参数化问题，导致翻译任务消耗了不必要的内存和计算资源。作者希望挖掘MoE架构中专家专业化与多语言能力可分离的特性，大幅压缩模型规模而不牺牲翻译质量。

Method: 利用MoE LLM中专家专业化（expert specialization）和多语言能力的可分离性，识别与翻译任务无关的专家；基于模块化特点直接剪枝这些专家，无需训练；进一步通过短时监督微调（SFT）恢复性能，实现更激进的压缩。

Result: 无需重新训练可剪掉50%专家且性能几乎无损，剪掉70%也仅出现轻微下降；经过短时SFT可剪掉75%专家并恢复基线性能，在部分设置下可剪掉近90%专家并保持合理的翻译质量。

Conclusion: 翻译任务只需要LLM的一小部分能力即可完成，针对MoE块的激进压缩（占总参数90%以上）是可行的，为LLM在特定任务上的轻量化部署提供了有效路径。

Abstract: Modern large language models (LLMs) achieve state-of-the-art machine translation performance, but they do so as broad generalists largely trained for many tasks and capabilities unrelated to translation. Thus, they are heavily overparameterized for this task, resulting in excessive memory and compute requirements. In this paper, we present a method for aggressively pruning experts from modern mixture-of-experts LLMs while incurring negligible degradation in translation quality. Our approach exploits expert specialization and the separability of multilingual capabilities in LLMs to identify experts irrelevant to translation. And because of the modular nature of MoEs, these can be easily pruned without any training. Without retraining, we are able to prune half of all experts with negligible degradation and 70% with only minor losses. With a very short SFT, we prune 75% of experts while recovering baseline performance, and in some settings remove nearly 90% while maintaining reasonable translation quality. Overall, our results show that translation requires only a fraction of the LLM, enabling substantial compression of the MoE blocks that contain over 90% of parameters.

</details>


### [131] [Knowledge Dependency Estimation for Reliable Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28047)
*Chaodong Tong,Qi Zhang,Nannan Sun,Lei Jiang,Yanbing Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Knot，一种结构化排序感知的知识依赖估计器，用于估计黑盒问答模型对不同知识单元的细粒度依赖程度，无需穷尽测试时扰动，并在多项选择和生成式QA基准上优于所有基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM-based QA中，知识来源多样（上下文、检索、分解、中间推理等），形成噪声大且冗余的候选空间，而非干净的黄金证据集。需要可靠的方法来估计模型对候选知识单元的依赖性，以识别正确答案依赖哪些知识，同时处理知识间的冗余性、可替代性和互补性。

Method: 提出Knot（结构化排序感知知识依赖估计器），通过子集级别的反事实监督进行学习，利用覆盖潜在依赖因子来建模子集敏感性，并推导出排序感知的单元级分数以识别有影响力的候选知识单元。

Result: 在多项选择和生成式QA基准上，Knot在子集敏感性预测上优于所有对比基线；在不额外调用QA模型的情况下，生成的单元排序比可部署基线更忠实；用于实际风险筛查时，依赖分数能有效提前标记易出错的QA预测。

Conclusion: Knot能够高效、准确地估计黑盒QA模型对知识单元的依赖关系，无需穷尽测试时扰动，并可用于实际风险筛查任务。

Abstract: Reliable question answering requires identifying not only whether an answer is correct, but also which available knowledge the prediction depends on. In realistic LLM-based QA, this knowledge may come from context, retrieval, decomposition, or intermediate reasoning, forming a noisy and redundant candidate space rather than a clean gold evidence set. We study \emph{knowledge dependency estimation}: estimating the sensitivity of a fixed black-box QA model to different candidate knowledge units. The challenge is to obtain fine-grained dependency scores without exhaustive test-time perturbation while modeling redundancy, substitutability, and complementarity. We propose \textbf{Knot}, a structured rank-aware knowledge dependency estimator. Knot learns from subset-level counterfactual supervision, models subset sensitivity through coverage over latent dependency factors, and derives rank-aware unit scores to identify influential candidates. Across multiple-choice and generative QA benchmarks, Knot outperforms all compared baselines in subset-sensitivity prediction and produces more faithful unit rankings than deployable baselines without extra QA-model calls; when used for practical risk screening, its dependency scores help flag error-prone QA predictions early.

</details>


### [132] [Prompting Is All You Need: Multi-view Prompting Large Language Models for Aspect-Based Sentiment Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28058)
*Nils Constantin Hellwig,Niklas Donhauser,Jakob Fehle,Udo Kruschwitz,Christian Wolff*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出LLM-MvP方法，通过多视图提示、模式约束解码、上下文无关文法和前缀批处理，在方面级情感分析任务中以少样本提示方式达到甚至超越微调模型的性能，同时大幅降低计算开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往LLM在ABSA任务中通过少样本提示虽优于零样本，但与微调模型仍存在性能差距，且LLM推理的计算成本过高，限制了实际部署。

Method: 提出LLM-MvP方法，包含三个关键技术：(1) 多视图提示——考虑多种元素排序；(2) 模式约束解码结合上下文无关文法；(3) 前缀批处理技术，三者协同降低计算开销并提升性能。

Result: 在五个基准数据集上的大量实验表明，LLM-MvP在少样本场景下达到了与微调模型竞争甚至更优的性能，同时大幅减少了计算开销。

Conclusion: LLM-MvP成功弥合了少样本提示与微调模型之间的性能差距，为ABSA提供了一种实用且高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Recent work explored the capabilities of Large Language Models (LLMs) in Aspect-Based Sentiment Analysis (ABSA) through few-shot prompting, requiring substantially fewer annotated examples while achieving notable improvements over zero-shot baselines. However, a performance gap remained compared to models fine-tuned on hundreds of examples, and the computational costs of LLM inference present practical barriers to deployment. We introduce LLM-based Multi-View Prompting (LLM-MvP), which adapts the multi-view principle of considering multiple element orderings to LLM prompting. By combining schema-constrained decoding with a context-free grammar and prefix batching, LLM-MvP achieves performance competitive or superior to fine-tuned approaches while substantially reducing computational overhead. Extensive experiments across five benchmark datasets demonstrate that LLM-MvP closes the gap between few-shot prompting and fine-tuned models, offering a practical and efficient solution for ABSA.

</details>


### [133] [Challenges in Explaining Pretrained Clinical Text Classifiers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28060)
*Kristian Miok,Matej Klemen,Blaz Škrlj,Marko Robnik Šikonja*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文指出在临床NLP中，基于token级别和扰动的解释方法（如LIME、SHAP）存在对非信息性token过度强调、归因不稳定以及高置信度预测不连贯输入等问题，强调需要开发临床有意义、语义基础扎实且对语言噪声鲁棒的解释策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床NLP中神经模型预测的解释仍是一个重大挑战，现有事后解释方法（如LIME、SHAP）在处理复杂且冗长的非结构化医学文本时表现不足。

Method: 通过在医院住院时长预测任务上进行针对性实验，识别token级别和基于扰动的解释技术的核心局限性。

Result: 发现这些方法存在三大问题：对非信息性token的过度强调、归因结果的不稳定性、以及对不连贯输入变体仍给出高置信度预测。

Conclusion: 现有解释策略不足以满足临床需求，亟需开发在临床上有意义、语义有基础且对语言噪声鲁棒的新型解释方法。

Abstract: Explaining the predictions of neural models in clinical NLP remains a significant challenge, especially for complex tasks involving long, unstructured medical texts. While post-hoc methods like LIME and SHAP are widely used, they often fall short when applied to clinical narratives. In this paper, we identify core limitations of token-level and perturbation-based explanation techniques through targeted demonstra- tions on a hospital length-of-stay prediction task. Our findings reveal issues such as overemphasis on non-informative tokens, instability in at- tributions, and high-confidence predictions for incoherent input variants. These results underscore the need for explanation strategies that are clin- ically meaningful, semantically grounded, and robust to linguistic noise.

</details>


### [134] [ConvMemory: A Lightweight Learned Memory Reranker, a Negative Attribution Result, and a Research-Preview Conflict Editor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28062)
*Taiheng Pan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ConvMemory是一个仅3.6M参数的学习型重排序器，通过融合稠密+词汇特征的交叉编码器蒸馏训练，用于对话长期记忆检索。在LongMemEval上以12-47倍低延迟超越BGE-large交叉编码器，比mxbai-rerank-large-v1便宜28倍但Recall差距在0.025以内。通过五种子重训练消融实验证明了其学习的时间窗口在统计上显著但并非时间特异性，真正的机制是廉价的交叉编码器蒸馏。同时发布了CCGE-LA候选集编辑器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对话系统的长期记忆检索需要高效且高质量的重排序器，现有方法在延迟或性能上存在不足，且此前有工作声称模型学习到了时间结构，但缺乏严格验证。

Method: 将小型双编码器模型通过交叉编码器教师蒸馏训练，融合稠密向量（来自教师模型）和词汇特征（如BM25）。通过五种子重训练消融及配对bootstrap检验，严格验证时间窗口机制的实际贡献。

Result: ConvMemory在LongMemEval上Recall@10接近或略逊于mxbai-rerank-large-v1，但延迟和成本显著降低。消融实验表明时间窗口机制对硬性非时间性控制样本影响最大，对多跳时间查询无显著效果。CCGE-LA在LoCoMo的supersession和stale/rescue切片上有小幅提升。

Conclusion: ConvMemory的有效机制是廉价的交叉编码器蒸馏与融合特征空间，而非时间结构利用。论文提供了诚实负面的归因结果，并发布了研究预览版CCGE-LA。但结果需独立复现审计。

Abstract: We describe ConvMemory, a small 3.6M-parameter learned reranker for conversational long-term memory retrieval, trained with cross-encoder teacher supervision over fused dense and lexical features. On the LongMemEval memory family, ConvMemory operates above the BGE-large cross-encoder in Recall@10 at 12-47x lower latency, remains within 0.025 Recall@10 of mxbai-rerank-large-v1 on Clean500 while running 28x cheaper; under Stress1000 distractors the Recall@10 gap widens to 0.081 but ConvMemory still operates at 117x lower latency; these LongMemEval numbers are single-run or single-seed and are reported as indicative cost-frontier evidence, not benchmark-grade. We then publish a rigorous negative attribution result on a previously claimed mechanism: a five-seed retrained ablation with paired bootstrap shows that ConvMemory's learned temporal window is statistically significant on aggregate but not temporally specific, with the largest effects on hard non-temporal controls and no significant effect on multi-hop temporal queries. The honest description of the mechanism is cheap cross-encoder distillation in a fused dense+lexical feature space, not temporal-structure exploitation. We additionally release CCGE-LA, a low-amplitude conflict-aware candidate-set editor over ConvMemory, as a research preview with modest but consistent gains on supersession and stale/rescue slices on LoCoMo. All results are retrieval-stage; ConvMemory does not match mxbai-rerank-large-v1 in absolute LoCoMo MRR, and the report is single-author and not yet independently audited.

</details>


### [135] [StoryLens: Preference-Aligned Story Rewriting via Context-Aware Narrative Enrichment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28073)
*Hanwen Cui,Yuting Mei,Yuhang Fu,Dingyi Yang,Qin Jin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出STORYLENSBENCH基准、STORYLENSEVAL奖励模型和STORYLENSWRITER两阶段重写模型，通过上下文感知的叙事丰富实现个性化故事重写，实验表明该方法在忠实度、连贯性和读者满意度上显著优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有故事重写工作仅关注表层风格迁移，忽略了上下文感知的叙事丰富对读者满意度的重要性。人类实验表明风格适配仅带来2.3%的满意度提升，而上下文增强重写则提升24.5%，因此需要构建面向偏好对齐的大规模故事重写基准和模型。

Method: 构建STORYLENSBENCH基准（包含结构化故事书、多维读者偏好画像和排序后的上下文感知重写故事）；提出STORYLENSEVAL奖励模型评估读者满意度；提出STORYLENSWRITER两阶段重写模型（监督微调+GRPO强化学习）。

Result: STORYLENSWRITER在忠实度、连贯性和读者满意度上一致优于强生成基线和个性化基线，验证了上下文感知叙事丰富对个性化故事重写的重要性。

Conclusion: 上下文感知的叙事丰富是提升个性化故事重写读者满意度的关键，而非单纯的风格迁移；所提出的基准、评估模型和重写模型为这一方向提供了坚实基础。

Abstract: Story rewriting aims to adapt existing narratives to diverse reader preferences while preserving plot consistency and narrative coherence. Unlike conventional work on style transfer, we argue that effective story rewriting demands context-aware narrative enrichment beyond surface-level stylistic adaptation. Our pilot human study shows that style adaptation alone provides only marginal gains in reader satisfaction (2.3%), while context-enhanced rewriting substantially improves user preference alignment (24.5%). Motivated by this, we introduce STORYLENSBENCH, a large-scale benchmark for preference-aligned story rewriting, comprising structured story books, multi-dimensional reader preference profiles, and ranked context-aware rewritten stories. Building on this benchmark, we propose STORYLENSEVAL, a reward model for estimating reader satisfaction over rewritten stories, and STORYLENSWRITER, a two-stage rewriting model combining supervised fine-tuning with GRPO-based reinforcement learning. We further establish a comprehensive evaluation framework covering fidelity, coherence, and reader satisfaction. Experimental results demonstrate that STORYLENSWRITER consistently outperforms strong generation and personalization baselines, highlighting the importance of context-aware narrative enrichment for personalized story rewriting.

</details>


### [136] [ATLAS: All-round Testing of Long-context Abilities across Scales](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28079)
*Deli Huang,Cunguang Wang,Hongyin Tang,Zhe Tang,Linsen Guo,Dongyu Ru,Ruoshi Yuan,Ziyue Zhu,Xiaoyu Li,Ziwen Wang,Chen Zhang,Anchun Gui,Wen Zan,Jiaqi Zhang,Xuezhi Cao,Jingang Wang,Xunliang Cai,Yixin Cao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ATLAS是一个重新定义长上下文评估的基准测试框架，提出分层分类法、长度感知AUC评分和ATLAScore三项方法论原则，在8个能力维度上评估了26个模型，发现模型排名在不同长度和任务类型间发生显著变化，表明长上下文质量应分能力和长度报告而非单一分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长上下文语言模型评估存在两大缺陷：(1)性能随长度增长可能崩溃但常被单一指标掩盖；(2)强检索能力未必能转移到下游任务。需要新的评估框架来系统性地刻画模型在不同长度和任务类型上的退化模式。

Method: 提出三层方法论：(i)分层分类法，将基础操作与应用工作负载分离以归因失败模式；(ii)长度感知AUC评分，在固定8K-1M网格上整合分数-长度曲线；(iii)ATLAScore，对各分类类目取调和平均，并通过端到端不确定性传播处理非线性聚合。实例化包含8个能力维度、9个可审计组件和6,438个实例。

Result: 评估26个模型发现：Gemini-3.1-Pro-Preview在128K领先，Claude-Opus-4.6在1M领先；ATLASscore@8K-128K和ATLASscore@8K-1M之间的排名显著变化，7个模型移动至少2位，两个分类层仅共享61%跨模型方差，个别排名差距高达12位。

Conclusion: 结果支持长上下文质量应按能力和长度分别报告，而非使用单一标题分数。ATLAS框架揭示了现有评估指标掩盖的关键失败模式，为长上下文模型提供了更细粒度、更可靠的比较基准。

Abstract: Long-context language models now advertise context windows up to millions of tokens, yet evaluations typically report a single length or a narrow task family, masking two failure modes: performance can collapse as length grows, and strong retrieval need not transfer to downstream use. We present ATLAS, a benchmarking framework that redefines long-context evaluation as length-dependent capability profiling. ATLAS contributes three methodological principles:(i) a layered taxonomy separating foundational operations from application workloads so failures can be attributed, (ii) length-aware AUC scoring that integrates score-length curves over a fixed 8K-1M grid, replacing single-point metrics with full degradation profiles, and (iii) ATLAScore, a harmonic-mean aggregate over taxonomy categories that penalizes imbalanced profiles, with end-to-end uncertainty propagation from subset scores through the nonlinear final aggregate. We instantiate the framework across eight capability dimensions with nine auditable components and 6,438 instances, and evaluate 26 models. Gemini-3.1-Pro-Preview leads at 128K, Claude-Opus-4.6 leads at 1M. Rankings reshuffle substantially between ATLASscore@8K-128K and ATLASscore@8K-1M: 7 models move by at least two ranks, and the two taxonomy layers share only 61% of cross-model variance, with individual rank gaps up to 12 positions. These results support reporting long-context quality by capability and length, not by a single headline score.

</details>


### [137] [ConRAG: Consensus-Driven Multi-View Retrieval for Multi-Hop Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28093)
*Yikai Zhu,Kunfeng Chen,Qihuang Zhong,Juhua Liu,Bo Du*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ConRAG是一种共识驱动的多视角RAG框架，通过系统优化查询端和语料库端，并利用多视角证据（关系、实体、文本信号）来提升大语言模型在复杂多跳问答任务上的表现，在三个基准上显著优于基线方法，最高比原始RAG提升26.9%，并使Gemma-4-31B在MuSiQue上达到新SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG方法在多跳问答任务上表现不佳，无论是查询端的任务分解方法还是语料端的知识图谱构建方法，都难以在复杂多跳QA任务上取得令人满意的性能。

Method: ConRAG框架的核心是共识驱动的多视图机制：1) 系统优化查询端和语料端；2) 利用多视角证据（关系信号、实体信号、文本信号）进行更精准的检索，通过多视图信息达成共识来增强检索质量。

Result: 在三个多跳QA基准上，ConRAG始终优于所有基线方法，平均性能比原始RAG提升高达26.9%，并使Gemma-4-31B在MuSiQue基准上创造了新的最佳记录。

Conclusion: ConRAG通过共识驱动的多视图框架有效解决了复杂多跳QA中的检索难题，验证了多视角证据融合对于提升多跳推理任务性能的重要价值。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) has emerged as a promising paradigm for enhancing large language models (LLMs) on multi-hop question answering (QA), which requires reasoning over evidence from multiple documents. Current multi-hop RAG methods generally focus on either query-side task decomposition or corpus-side knowledge graph construction. Despite their progress, these methods still struggle to achieve satisfactory performance on complex multi-hop QA tasks. To this end, we propose ConRAG, a consensus-driven multi-view RAG framework that effectively boosts LLMs on complex multi-hop QA. The core of ConRAG is to systematically optimize both the query and corpus sides and to leverage multi-view evidence (relation, entity, and text signals) for more accurate retrieval. Extensive experiments on three multi-hop QA benchmarks show that ConRAG consistently outperforms all baselines by a clear margin, e.g., up to +26.9% average performance gains over vanilla RAG, and enables Gemma-4-31B to achieve a new state-of-the-art record on the challenging MuSiQue benchmark.

</details>


### [138] [LegalGraphRAG: Multi-Agent Graph Retrieval-Augmented Generation for Reliable Legal Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28120)
*Zerui Chen,Qinggang Zhang,Zhishang Xiang,Zhimin Wei,Linfeng Gao,Xiao Huang,Zhihong Zhang,Jinsong Su*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: LegalGraphRAG是一个专为法律推理设计的框架，通过层次化法律图结构组织多粒度法律知识，并结合多智能体系统（研究员-审计员-裁判员）实现可验证、可信赖的法律判决，在实验中达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GraphRAG在处理法律领域时面临两大挑战：(i) 法律语料异质性强，包含案例、法条、解释等多粒度知识，平面知识图谱无法区分事实细节、适用规则和抽象原则；(ii) 法律判决需要透明、基于证据的推理，而传统RAG直接将检索结果传给LLM缺乏验证，导致推理不透明且易出错。

Method: 提出LegalGraphRAG框架，包含两个核心组件：(1) 层次化法律图（Hierarchical Legal Graph），将法律来源按层次组织，支持在适当的抽象层级进行检索；(2) 多智能体系统，包含Researcher（检索候选证据）、Auditor（严格验证证据与源文档的一致性）、Adjudicator（综合已验证证据作出最终判决）。

Result: 大量实验表明，LegalGraphRAG在准确性和可信度方面均达到SOTA性能，优于现有GraphRAG基线方法。

Conclusion: LegalGraphRAG通过层次化图结构解决了法律知识异质性问题，通过多智能体验证机制增强了法律推理的可信度和透明度，为法律领域的可靠推理提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Graph-based Retrieval-Augmented Generation (GraphRAG) advances flat document retrieval by structuring knowledge as relational graphs, enabling more coherent and effective reasoning. However, applying it to specific domains like legal reasoning faces critical challenges. (i) Legal corpora are heterogeneous, containing multi-granular knowledge from cases, articles and interpretations. A flat knowledge graph cannot adequately differentiate between factual details, applied rules, and abstract principles, limiting accurate retrieval. (ii) Reliable legal judgment demands transparent, evidence-based reasoning. Traditional RAG passes retrieved context directly to an LLM without verification, resulting in opaque, error-prone reasoning. To this end, we propose LegalGraphRAG, a framework designed for reliable legal reasoning. Our approach introduces two core components: a hierarchical legal graph that hierarchically organizes legal sources to enable retrieval at appropriate abstraction levels, and a multi-agent system for reliable legal reasoning, where a Researcher retrieves candidate evidence, an Auditor rigorously verifies its validity against source documents, and an Adjudicator synthesizes the set of verified evidence to render a final judgment. Extensive experiments show that LegalGraphRAG achieves the state-of-the-art performance, outperforming existing GraphRAG baselines in accurate and trustworthy legal analysis. Our code, datasets and implementation details are available at https://github.com/XMUDeepLIT/LegalGraphRAG.

</details>


### [139] [Risk-aware Selective Prompting for Hallucination Mitigation in Large Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28123)
*Yuang Huang,Yafeng Zhang,Yu Zilan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了提示验证（Prompt-based verification）在大型视觉语言模型（LVLMs）中的效果，发现其效果与输入难度相关：对困难输入有帮助，但对简单输入可能有害。原因是验证提示会导致注意力从视觉标记转向指令标记，造成保守输出偏移。为此提出无训练的风险感知选择性提示（RSP）方法，利用生成前不确定性信号选择性触发验证，在保持基线性能的同时缓解持续提示的性能下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提示验证被广泛用于缓解LVLMs的幻觉问题，但何时有效仍不清楚，缺乏系统性的理解和分析。

Method: 1) 系统分析验证提示在两种代表性LVLM架构上的效果，按输入难度分层评估；2) 分析注意力分布和熵模式，揭示验证提示的机制；3) 提出RSP方法，利用生成前不确定性信号选择性触发验证。

Result: 验证提示的纠正随输入难度增加而增加，但新引入的错误在各难度水平下持续存在；验证提示导致注意力从视觉标记转向指令标记，并产生独特的中层熵模式；RSP方法能有效缓解持续提示的负面影响，且不同架构有效选择信号不同。

Conclusion: 验证提示并非总是有益，其效果取决于输入难度。RSP提供了一种训练无关的解决方案，可根据输入特征选择性应用验证，为提升LVLMs可靠性提供了新思路。

Abstract: Prompt-based verification is widely used to mitigate hallucinations in large vision-language models (LVLMs), yet when it helps remains poorly understood. We systematically study verification prompting across two representative LVLM architectures and hallucination benchmarks, and find that it is a risk-bearing intervention: its corrections increase with input difficulty, while newly introduced errors persist across difficulty levels. As a result, always-on prompting helps on hard inputs but offers little benefit -- and can harm -- easier ones. Our analysis further shows that this behavior is associated with a conservative output shift. Verification prompts redistribute attention from visual tokens toward instruction tokens and induce a distinct middle-layer entropy pattern absent in a neutral-prompt control, suggesting instruction-conditioned attention redistribution rather than uniformly improved visual grounding. Motivated by this input-dependent risk, we propose Risk-aware Selective Prompting (RSP), a training-free approach that uses pre-generation uncertainty signals to trigger verification selectively. RSP mitigates the degradation of always-on prompting while preserving baseline performance, and reveals that effective selection signals vary across architectures.

</details>


### [140] [Chinese Word Boundary Recovery through Character Alignment Projection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28128)
*Lusha Wang,Yuchen Li,Su Yuan,Jungyeul Park*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出中文分词边界恢复的对齐投影方法，通过将噪声源句与干净目标句进行字符级对齐，再将目标侧词边界投影回源句，以解决非标准文本（如语言学习者错误）中的分词脆弱性问题。实验表明该方法能纠正过度切分错误，并提供了两个评估基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中文分词在非标准文本（如语言学习者错误导致的字符级偏离）中表现脆弱，这些错误破坏了下游标注和评估所依赖的词边界，亟需一种鲁棒的词边界恢复机制。

Method: 将词边界恢复形式化为基于对齐的投影任务：先对噪声源句与干净目标句进行字符级对齐，再将目标侧词边界投影回源句，从而恢复源句的词边界。

Result: 直接分词在复合词碎片化问题上表现脆弱，而两步投影方法通过利用校正后的目标句恢复源侧词跨度，有效纠正了过度切分错误。

Conclusion: 词边界恢复与普通分词存在本质差异，对齐投影为在噪声输入下稳定中文标注和评估提供了一种有原则的机制。

Abstract: Chinese word segmentation is especially fragile in non-standard text, where language learner errors and other character-level divergences disrupt the word boundaries assumed by downstream annotation and evaluation. This paper formulates Chinese word boundary recovery as an alignment-based projection task. Given a noisy source sentence and a cleaner target counterpart, we first align the two strings at the character level and then project target-side word boundaries back onto the source. Beyond the recovery method itself, we introduce two evaluation resources: a manually checked learner Chinese benchmark based on MuCGEC and a controlled synthetic benchmark derived from the Chinese Penn Treebank. Experiments show that direct segmentation remains vulnerable to compound fragmentation in learner input, whereas the proposed two step projection method corrects many over-segmentation errors by using the corrected target to recover source-side word spans. The results show that word boundary recovery is distinct from ordinary segmentation and that alignment projection provides a principled mechanism for stabilizing Chinese annotation and evaluation under noisy input.

</details>


### [141] [Better heads do not guarantee better binarized constituency parsing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28131)
*Zeyao Qi,Yige Chen,Eitan Klinger,Vivaan Wadhwa,Jungyeul Park*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文重新审视了标点感知的树二值化方法，探究基于依存关系的中心词信息能否提升二值化解析器的监督效果。研究发现，尽管学习得到的中心词在内在中心词预测任务上显著优于基于规则的基线，但在解析性能（反二值化后）上并未带来一致增益。特别是在考虑标点符号的条件下，学习中心词在宏观平均标点敏感F1上甚至不如规则二值化方法。类似的不稳定性在跨树库迁移中也存在。结果表明，语言学上合理的中心词信息作为二值化控制信号时，未必是解析器最优的。本文呈现了一个负面结果：更好的中心词预测并不等价于更好的标点敏感成分句法分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机在于检验一个隐含假设：使用更准确的中心词（特别是基于依存关系学习的中心词）来指导树二值化，是否能提升成分句法分析器对标点符号敏感场景下的解析性能。尽管学习中心词在内在任务上表现优异，但其对下游解析的影响尚未被系统地、标点敏感地评估。

Method: 论文采用对比实验范式，将学习得到的中心词与基于规则的中心词分别用于树的二值化，随后训练解析器并反二值化恢复树结构。特别设计了标点条件化评估（punctuation-conditioned evaluation），在CTB等树库上比较两种二值化策略在标点敏感F1指标上的表现，同时在跨树库迁移场景下验证稳定性。

Result: 1) 学习中心词在内在中心词预测任务上显著优于规则中心词；2) 在解析器反二值化后，学习中心词并未带来一致的F1增益，整体性能提升有限且不稳定；3) 在标点条件化评估下，学习中心词在宏观平均标点敏感F1上反而低于规则二值化方法；4) 类似的不稳定性在跨树库迁移（cross-treebank transfer）中同样出现。

Conclusion: 语言学导向的中心词信息（即使更准确）并不必然是解析器二值化监督的最优选择；更好的中心词预测不必然带来更好的标点敏感成分句法分析性能。论文呈现了一个负面结果，提示研究者在设计二值化策略时需要谨慎对待中心词选择与下游任务目标之间的不一致性。

Abstract: We revisit punctuation-aware tree binarization for constituency parsing and ask whether dependency-induced headedness improves binary parser supervision. Although learned heads substantially outperform rule-based heads in intrinsic head prediction, they do not yield consistent parsing gains after debinarization. In particular, punctuation-conditioned evaluation shows that learned headedness underperforms rule-based binarization in macro-average punctuation-sensitive $F_1$, despite a small overall gain on CTB. Similar instability appears under cross-treebank transfer. These results suggest that \ycc{linguistically grounded} headedness is not necessarily parser-optimal when used as a binarization control signal. The paper presents a negative result: better head prediction does not imply better punctuation-sensitive constituency parsing.

</details>


### [142] [DEPART: DEcomposing PARiTy across Multilingual LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28163)
*Manan Uppadhyay,Prashant Kodali,Pranjal Chitale,Reshma Ramaprasad,Himanshu Beniwal,Sunayana Sitaram*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出两阶段贝叶斯层次框架，将多语言大模型的性能方差分解为可解释成分：语言特征（文字、语系、类型距离）解释理解任务79%和推理任务92%的语言方差，其中模型内部表征与英语的相似性是最强预测因子；完整方差分解显示模型身份主导理解（66.7%），而基准×模型交互主导推理（46.3%），将多语言评估从被动性能映射转变为可解释的诊断框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多语言大模型排行榜虽报告各语言准确率，但极少解释性能差异的根源，导致系统性偏差无法归因，从业者也缺乏可操作的控制手段。

Method: 首先通过无分布假设的Friedman和Kruskal-Wallis检验确认语言差距的系统性；然后引入两阶段贝叶斯层次框架：(1) 隔离语言身份方差并量化可观测语言特征（文字、语系、类型距离、模型内部表征与英语的相似性）的解释力；(2) 对完整模型×基准×语言立方体进行方差分解。

Result: 语言特征解释理解任务79%和推理任务92%的语言方差；模型表征与英语的相似性是最强预测因子；模型身份主导理解任务方差（66.7%），基准×模型交互主导推理任务方差（46.3%）。

Conclusion: 多语言评估应从被动性能映射转向可解释的诊断框架，提供针对语言差异根源的具体控制杠杆。

Abstract: Multilingual Large Language Models (mLLMs) leaderboards report per-language accuracy but rarely explain why disparities emerge, leaving systemic biases unattributed and offering practitioners no actionable levers. We first establish that these gaps are systematic rather than artifacts of sampling noise via distribution-free Friedman and Kruskal--Wallis tests, then introduce a two-step Bayesian hierarchical framework that decomposes multilingual performance variance into interpretable components. First, isolating the variance attributable to language identity, we show that observable language features (script, family, typological distance) explain $R^2_{\text{ling}} = 79\%$ of this variance on understanding tasks and $92\%$ on reasoning, with a model's internal representational similarity to English emerging as the dominant predictor across both task buckets. Second, decomposing the full (model$\times$benchmark$\times$language) cube, we find that NLU and reasoning have fundamentally divergent variance profiles: model identity dominates understanding ($66.7\%$ of variance), whereas the benchmark$\times$model interaction dominates reasoning ($46.3\%$). Together these results recast multilingual evaluation from passive performance mapping into an explainable, diagnostic framework with concrete levers for targeting the root drivers of language disparity.

</details>


### [143] [SuperValid: Capability-Aligned OOD Validation for Generalizable Downstream Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28179)
*Quanen Sun,Changxin Tian,Ke Shi,Cai Chen,Cunyin Peng,Jia Liu,Kunlong Chen,Zhiqiang Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出SuperValid框架，通过合成能力级别的OOD验证数据来预测大语言模型的下游性能，克服了基准级和IID验证损失的泛化限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有缩放律研究在预测下游性能时存在两方面泛化局限：基准级预测引入场景特定噪声，而IID验证损失在训练分布变化时无法追踪能力提升。因此需要一种更稳健的下游性能预测方法。

Method: 提出SuperValid框架，从基准测试中蒸馏能力域的核心概念，并扩展为多样化、知识丰富的OOD文本，合成与能力对齐的验证数据。在6个能力域17个基准测试上验证。

Result: SuperValid损失与不同架构、规模、训练数据分布模型的下游性能展现出强且稳定的相关性。作为一种无需基准评估、训练中可计算的指标，能有效指导模型选择、早停和缩放决策。

Conclusion: 能力级别的下游缩放律研究比基准级更有效，SuperValid提供了一种训练无关、可在线计算的性能预测方法。

Abstract: Scaling laws guide large language model training by relating compute to cross-entropy loss, and recent work further extends them to predict downstream benchmark performance. However, prior approaches face generalization limitations from two aspects: focusing on benchmark-level performance introduces scenario-specific artifacts, while relying on IID validation loss fails to track capability improvements when training distributions vary. In this work, we argue that downstream scaling should be studied at the capability level, which captures shared skill factors across related tasks while abstracting away benchmark-specific noise. We propose SuperValid, a framework that synthesizes OOD (out-of-distribution), capability-aligned validation data by distilling core concepts from benchmarks within a capability domain and expanding them into diverse, knowledge-rich texts. Extensive experiments spanning 17 benchmarks grouped into 6 capability domains show that SuperValid loss exhibits strong and stable correlation with downstream performance across models of different architectures, scales, and training data distributions. As a training-free metric computable during training without benchmark evaluation, SuperValid enables effective model selection, early stopping, and scaling decisions.

</details>


### [144] [When Confidence Misleads: Suffix Anchoring and Anchor-Proximity Confidence Modulation for Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28181)
*Jungwon Park,Jimyeong Kim,Jungmin Ko,Nojun Kwak,Wonjong Rhee*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了扩散语言模型中完全非自回归解码的置信度误导问题，发现EOT token的高置信度会导致生成不完整，而插入后缀锚点虽能缓解此问题但会引发锚点附近token的过早解码。为此提出后缀锚点置信度调制方法，在保持后缀锚点生成完成优势的同时，根据解码进度调制锚点附近置信度，在文本推理、视觉-语言推理和代码生成基准上持续提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散语言模型在完全非自回归解码时，基于置信度的位置选择策略存在两个关键问题：一是EOT token获得高置信度导致生成不完整；二是为解决此问题引入的后缀锚点方法会导致锚点附近token因局部过置信而被过早解码。作者旨在修正置信度误导问题，实现更可靠的推理时解码策略。

Method: 提出后缀锚点置信度调制方法，这是一种无需训练的解码策略：1) 插入短后缀锚点以鼓励响应完成；2) 根据解码进度动态调制锚点附近token的置信度，降低锚点邻近位置的置信度估值，从而抑制过早解码，同时保留后缀锚点促进完整生成的优势。

Result: 在文本推理、视觉-语言推理和代码生成三个基准上，该方法一致优于基于置信度的完全非自回归基线，超过显式EOT抑制方法，并保持完全非自回归生成的并行解码优势。

Conclusion: 置信度在扩散语言模型的完全非自回归解码中不可靠，通过后缀锚点置信度调制可以有效缓解EOT误导和局部过置信问题，是一种简单有效的训练无关改进方案。

Abstract: Diffusion language models decode text by iteratively denoising masked token sequences, making the choice of which positions to decode a central inference-time decision. Most training-free decoding strategies use model confidence for position selection, assuming that high-confidence positions are ready to be decoded. In this work, we revisit this assumption by studying when confidence misleads fully non-autoregressive (fully non-AR) decoding. EOT tokens can receive high confidence and cause incomplete generation; inserting a suffix anchor can mitigate this issue but introduces local overconfidence near the anchor, causing anchor-adjacent tokens to be decoded too early. To address these issues, we propose Suffix-Anchored Confidence Modulation, a simple training-free method that inserts a short suffix anchor to encourage response completion and modulates confidence near the anchor according to decoding progress. This preserves the response-completion benefit of suffix anchoring while reducing premature decoding of anchor-adjacent tokens. Across text-only reasoning, vision-language reasoning, and code-generation benchmarks, our method consistently improves confidence-based fully non-AR decoding, outperforms explicit EOT suppression, and preserves the parallel decoding advantage of fully non-AR generation.

</details>


### [145] [BenGER: Benchmarking LLM Systems on Subsumption-Based Legal Reasoning in German Law](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28183)
*Sebastian Nagl,Ann-Kristin Mayrhofer,Martin Heidebach,Aleyna Koçak,Anne Zettelmeier,Elly Breu,Angelina Greiner,Sofija Milijas,Matthias Grabmair*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了BenGER数据集，用于评估大语言模型在德国法律中的法律推理能力，并发现封闭旗舰模型表现最佳，人机协作显著优于纯人工工作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有法律AI评估缺乏针对德国法律体系下法律归摄推理（subsumption-based legal reasoning）的标准化基准，需要构建专门数据集来衡量LLM在该领域的实际能力。

Method: 构建了包含596个法律案例任务和531个教义推理任务的BenGER数据集；评估了12种LLM系统；采用自动评估和基于法官的评估指标；引入基于评分标准的LLM-as-a-Judge框架，并与多评审者人工评分协议交叉验证。

Result: LLM法官替代盲审人工评审者时，与完整人工评审池的一致性几乎不降低（Calderon r=0.96 vs. r=0.96, n=30）；封闭旗舰模型在所有语料库中领先；人机协作显著优于纯人工工作。

Conclusion: BenGER可作为德国法律推理评估的可靠基准；LLM法官可以提供与人工评审高度一致的评估；人机协作模式在法律推理任务中具有明显优势。

Abstract: We introduce the BenGER (Benchmark for German Law) dataset for evaluating LLM systems on subsumption-based legal reasoning in German law. The BenGER dataset consists of three components: 596 exam-style free-text legal case tasks across multiple levels of legal education and 531 short doctrinal reasoning tasks. We evaluate 12 contemporary LLM systems -- closed flagship, efficiency-oriented, and open-weight -- across automatic and judge-based metrics. On a controlled validation subset of timed human-written solutions under both unaided and human--AI co-creation conditions, we contextualise model performance against these human baselines. We introduce a rubric-aligned LLM-as-a-Judge framework cross-validated against a multi-rater human-grading protocol (three blind reviews plus one author-informed creator review per solution). Our results show that replacing a blind human reviewer with the LLM judge degrades agreement with the full human pool no more than removing that reviewer altogether (Calderon r=0.96 vs.~r=0.96, matched n=30), that closed-flagship systems lead the leaderboard across all corpora, and that human--AI co-creation substantially outperforms unaided human work.

</details>


### [146] [Framing Matters: Addressing Framing Sensitivity in Decision-Making through Behaviorally-Grounded Value Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28188)
*Seojin Hwang,Minju Kim,Junhyuk Choi,JeongHyun Park,Hwanhee Lee*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文发现大语言模型在面对事实相同但框架不同的输入时，决策翻转率高达28.6%，并提出了一个名为Fragile的基准数据集和一种名为Valign的表示级方法，通过锚定价值先验、引导隐藏状态向价值一致方向投影、移除时间-生动性敏感方向，有效减少框架诱导的决策不一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM被部署在法律推理等高风险场景中，事实等价的输入应产生一致决策，但现有模型对语义框架敏感，导致决策不稳定，缺乏系统性研究该问题的基准和缓解方法。
